  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学功能源于序列、结构、调控、进化和细胞环境等多模态约束的耦合，但现有基础模型通常只在单一模态或固定前向任务上训练，缺乏统一的多模态生成式框架。

Method: 构建了多模态对齐数据集LORE，链接核酸、蛋白质、进化、结构、调控和语义/语境模态；采用分裂轨道编码器-解码器架构，可条件性地处理任意子集的观测模态，重建或生成缺失的分子状态组分。

Result: 多模态条件输入持续改善序列重建性能；在RNA和蛋白质下游任务上达到最优；实现最优剪接预测且异构体感知推理进一步提升性能；在临床相关HBB剪接破坏突变中识别纠正编辑；在PD-L1和hACE2结合位点设计多样高置信度序列；实现实验语境依赖的RNA化学探测建模。

Conclusion: MIMIC的对齐多模态生成式建模为统一表征学习、条件预测和约束生物分子设计提供了强大的单一模型基础框架。

Abstract: Biological function emerges from coupled constraints across sequence, structure, regulation, evolution, and cellular context, yet most foundation models in biology are trained within one modality or for a fixed forward task. We present MIMIC, a generative multimodal foundation model trained on our newly curated and aligned dataset, LORE, linking nucleic acid, protein, evolutionary, structural, regulatory, and semantic/contextual modalities within partially observed biomolecular states. MIMIC uses a split-track encoder-decoder architecture to condition on arbitrary subsets of observed modalities and reconstruct or generate missing components of molecular state across the genome, transcriptome, and proteome. Multimodal conditioning consistently improves MIMIC's sequence reconstruction relative to sequence-only inputs, while its learned representations enable state-of-the-art performance on RNA and protein downstream tasks. MIMIC achieves state-of-the-art splicing prediction, and its joint generative formulation enables isoform-aware inference that further improves performance. Beyond prediction, the same generative framework supports constrained design. For RNA, MIMIC identifies corrective edits in a clinically relevant HBB splice-disrupting mutation without reverting it by using evolutionary and structural signals. For proteins, jointly conditioning on shape and surface chemistry of PD-L1 and hACE2 binding sites produces diverse, high-confidence sequences with strong in silico support for target binding. Finally, MIMIC uses experimental context as semantic conditioning to model assay-dependent RNA chemical probing, rather than treating context as a fixed output. Together, these results position MIMIC's aligned multimodal generative modeling as a strong foundation for unifying representation learning, conditional prediction, and constrained biomolecular design within a single model.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond the Attention Stability Boundary: Agentic Self-Synthesizing Reasoning Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24512)
*Dahlia Shehata,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"注意力锁存"(Attention Latch)的失效模式，这是解码器-only自回归Transformer在多轮对话中出现的系统性问题，源于历史上下文的累积概率权重压制了中途更新指令。为解决该问题，论文提出了自合成推理协议(SSRP)，一种将高层架构规划(Architect)与逐轮执行(Executive)分离的元认知框架。在MultiWOZ 2.2数据集上的实验表明，当基线模型成功率降至0.1%时，SSRP实现了715倍的弹性提升，并在多个前沿模型中验证了统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在向自主数字协作者演进时，非线性多轮对话中保持确定性的目标导向性成为架构瓶颈。现有方法无法解决历史上下文概率权重覆盖中途任务更新的问题，导致智能体锚定于过时约束。

Method: 提出Self-Synthesizing Reasoning Protocols (SSRP)元认知框架，包含架构规划器(Architect)和逐轮执行器(Executive)的离散分离。引入Aggregate Pivot Accuracy (APA)新评估指标，通过映射到U型"Lost in the Middle"曲线验证其有效性。实验包括三个层级：浅层近因检索、高熵标准操作程序、语义劫持3跳多事实合成任务。

Result: SSRP在基线模型(如GPT 5.4的Vanilla ReAct)成功率降至0.1%时实现715倍弹性提升。在Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.6、DeepSeek V3.2上均取得统计显著增益。审计证实注意力锁存的存在，并通过等距压力测试(90%准确率)解耦位置偏差。程序完整性审计达98.8% adherence，并揭示了"基础悖论"(Grounding Paradox)。

Conclusion: SSRP有效解决了自回归Transformer中的注意力锁存问题，通过元认知框架实现了架构规划与执行的分离，在极端条件下表现出极高的弹性。信息瓶颈原理和粒度消融实验为SSRP提供了理论形式化支撑。研究还揭示了高稳定性模型因拒绝在检索-推理污染下产生幻觉而失效的悖论现象。

Abstract: As LLM agents transition to autonomous digital coworkers, maintaining deterministic goal-directedness in non-linear multi-turn conversations emerged as an architectural bottleneck. We identify and formalize a systemic failure mode termed the Attention Latch in decoder-only autoregressive Transformers. This phenomenon, a behavioral manifestation of Information Over-squashing, occurs when the cumulative probabilistic weight of historical context overrides mid-task updates, causing agents to remain anchored to obsolete constraints despite explicit contradictory instructions. We propose Self-Synthesizing Reasoning Protocols (SSRP), a metacognitive framework that implements a discrete separation between high-level architectural planning (Architect) and turn-by-turn procedural execution (Executive). We evaluate SSRP across 9K trajectories using the MultiWOZ 2.2 dataset and the Aggregate Pivot Accuracy (APA), a novel metric we validate by mapping its scores to the U-shaped 'Lost in the Middle' curve. We present 3 experimental tiers: a shallow recency-based retrieval pilot, a high-entropy SOP, and a semantic hijacked 3-hop Multi-Fact Synthesis task. Our results empirically locate the Attention Stability Boundary, where stateless Vanilla ReAct baselines for GPT 5.4 collapse to 0.1% success while SSRP achieves a 715X Resilience Lift. We demonstrate statistically significant gains across Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.6 and DeepSeek V3.2. Audits confirm SSRP necessity by proving attentional lapse via a recursive reflexion baseline (100% success); decoupling the latch from positional bias through equidistant stress testing (90% accuracy); and formalizing SSRP via the Information Bottleneck principle and granularity ablations. Procedural Integrity audit (98.8% adherence) reveals a Grounding Paradox where high-stability models fail by refusing to hallucinate under retrieval-reasoning contamination.

</details>


### [149] [STELLAR-E: a Synthetic, Tailored, End-to-end LLM Application Rigorous Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24544)
*Alessio Sordo,Lingxiao Du,Meeka-Hanna Lenisa,Evgeny Bogdanov,Maxim Romanovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STELLAR-E是一个全自动系统，能在无需依赖现有数据集的情况下，通过最小人工输入生成高质量、可定制规模的合成数据集，用于评估大语言模型。系统包含两阶段：改进的TGRT Self-Instruct合成数据引擎和评估管道。合成数据集在LLM-as-a-judge评分上比现有语言特定基准平均高出5.7%，证明了其可比质量，但真实数据集对LLM（尤其小模型）仍更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同领域的广泛部署需要高质量的领域特定和语言特定评估数据集，但手动收集这些数据集面临隐私问题、法规限制和时间成本高等困难。现有的自动化基准方法存在依赖已有数据、可扩展性差、单领域聚焦、缺乏多语言支持等局限性。

Method: 系统分为两阶段：(1) 修改TGRT Self-Instruct框架构建合成数据引擎，实现可控的、可定制的合成数据集生成；(2) 构建评估管道，结合统计指标和基于LLM的指标，评估合成数据集在LLM应用评估中的适用性。

Result: 合成数据集在LLM-as-a-judge评分上比现有语言特定基准平均高出5.7%，证明其质量可比。但真实数据集对LLM（尤其是小模型）仍更具挑战性。

Conclusion: STELLAR-E建立了一个可扩展、领域自适应的基准框架，支持公平评估LLM应用，提供了比手动方法更快的替代方案，实现了高效自动化的质量保障循环。

Abstract: The increasing reliance on Large Language Models (LLMs) across diverse sectors highlights the need for robust domain-specific and language-specific evaluation datasets; however, the collection of such datasets is challenging due to privacy concerns, regulatory restrictions, and the time cost for manual creation. Existing automated benchmarking methods are often limited by relying on pre-existing data, poor scalability, single-domain focus, and lack of multilingual support. We present STELLAR-E - a fully automated system to generate high-quality synthetic datasets of custom size, using minimal human inputs without depending on existing datasets. The system is structured in two stages: (1) We modify the TGRT Self-Instruct framework to create a synthetic data engine that enables controllable, custom synthetic dataset generation, and (2) an evaluation pipeline incorporating statistical and LLM-based metrics to assess the applicability of the synthetic dataset for LLM-based application evaluations. The synthetic datasets reach an average difference of +5.7% in terms of LLM-as-a-judge scores against existing language-specific benchmarks, demonstrating comparable quality for comprehensive assessment of big and small LLMs. While real datasets remain slightly more challenging for LLMs especially for smaller models, this work establishes a scalable and domain-adaptable benchmarking framework that supports fair evaluation of LLM applications, offering a faster alternative to manual approaches and enabling high-efficiency automated quality assurance cycles.

</details>


### [150] [Hierarchical Behaviour Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24558)
*Michael Tryfan Matthews,Anssi Kanervisto,Jakob Foerster,Pierluca D'Oro,Scott Fujimoto,Mikael Henaff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层行为空间（HBS）方法，通过让控制器指定奖励函数的线性组合，而非为每个选项使用单一奖励函数，从而诱导出更丰富的行为空间。在NetHack学习环境上的实验表明，该方法性能优异，且分层带来的收益主要源于增强的探索能力，而非长期推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习工作中，每个选项（option）使用单一的奖励函数，限制了策略的表达能力。本文旨在通过允许控制器指定奖励函数的线性组合，使选项能够表示更广泛的行为空间，从而提高分层强化学习的性能。

Method: 提出分层行为空间（HBS）方法，核心思想是让高层控制器为每个选项指定一个奖励函数的线性组合（而非单一奖励函数），从而在选项层面诱导出连续的行为空间，使底层策略能够表达更丰富的策略。

Result: 在NetHack学习环境上的实验表明，HBS方法取得了优异的性能。进一步的实验分析发现，分层带来的性能提升主要源于增加了探索能力，而非改善了长期推理能力，这一发现与常规认知有所不同。

Conclusion: HBS通过奖励函数线性组合的方式有效扩展了分层强化学习中选项的行为表达能力，在复杂环境中表现良好。关键洞见是分层的收益主要归因于探索增强，这为未来分层强化学习的设计提供了新的理解。

Abstract: Recent work in hierarchical reinforcement learning has shown success in scaling to billions of timesteps when learning over a set of predefined option reward functions. We show that, instead of using a single reward function per option, the reward functions can be effectively used to induce a space of behaviours, by letting the controller specify linear combinations over reward functions, allowing a more expressive set of policies to be represented. We call this method Hierarchical Behaviour Spaces (HBS). We evaluate HBS on the NetHack Learning Environment, demonstrating strong performance. We conduct a series of experiments and determine that, perhaps going against conventional wisdom, the benefits of hierarchy in our method come from increased exploration rather than long term reasoning.

</details>


### [151] [Towards Lawful Autonomous Driving: Deriving Scenario-Aware Driving Requirements from Traffic Laws and Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24562)
*Bowen Jian,Rongjie Yu,Hong Wang,Liqiang Wang,Zihang Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交通场景分类法的LLM推理管道，通过层次化语义锚点将法律与场景精确匹配，在5,897个场景中将法律-场景匹配率提升29.1%，派生强制性需求和禁止性需求准确率分别提升36.9%和38.2%，并构建了自动驾驶法律合规层与实时监控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于形式逻辑的自动驾驶合规方法劳动密集、难以扩展且维护成本高；LLM虽可用于从交通法规推导要求，但在缺乏结构化场景推理时容易检索不相关或遗漏适用条款，导致推导不精确。

Method: 提出一种新管道，通过节点式锚点（编码层次语义）将LLM推理锚定在交通场景分类法中，实现法律与场景的精确匹配与需求推导。

Result: 在中国交通法规与OnSite数据集（5,897个场景）上，法律-场景匹配提升29.1%，强制性需求准确率提升36.9%，禁止性需求准确率提升38.2%；构建了自动驾驶合规层与车载实时合规监控器。

Conclusion: 该方法为自动驾驶的法规合规编码提供了可扩展、精确的解决方案，为未来AV开发、部署与监管奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Driving in compliance with traffic laws and regulations is a basic requirement for human drivers, yet autonomous vehicles (AVs) can violate these requirements in diverse real-world scenarios. To encode law compliance into AV systems, conventional approaches use formal logic languages to explicitly specify behavioral constraints, but this process is labor-intensive, hard to scale, and costly to maintain. With recent advances in artificial intelligence, it is promising to leverage large language models (LLMs) to derive legal requirements from traffic laws and regulations. However, without explicitly grounding and reasoning in structured traffic scenarios, LLMs often retrieve irrelevant provisions or miss applicable ones, yielding imprecise requirements. To address this, we propose a novel pipeline that grounds LLM reasoning in a traffic scenario taxonomy through node-wise anchors that encode hierarchical semantics. On Chinese traffic laws and OnSite dataset (5,897 scenarios), our method improves law-scenario matching by 29.1\% and increases the accuracy of derived mandatory and prohibitive requirements by 36.9\% and 38.2\%, respectively. We further demonstrate real-world applicability by constructing a law-compliance layer for AV navigation and developing an onboard, real-time compliance monitor for in-field testing, providing a solid foundation for future AV development, deployment, and regulatory oversight.

</details>


### [152] [FastOMOP: A Foundational Architecture for Reliable Agentic Real-World Evidence Generation on OMOP CDM data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24572)
*Niko Moeller-Grell,Shihao Shenzhang,Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Richard JB Dobson,Vishnu V Chandrabalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FastOMOP 是一种开源多智能体架构，通过分离治理层、可观测层和编排层与可插拔智能体团队，实现了受治理的 RWE 自动化生成。在三个 OMOP CDM 数据集上验证表明，过程边界治理能确保安全性独立于模型选择，可靠性达 0.84-0.94，对抗性和越界拦截率完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RWE 生成依赖人工流程，LLM 和多智能体系统虽有望自动化，但引入涌现行为、协调失败和安全风险，且缺乏覆盖全生命周期的灵活、安全、可审计的基础设施。

Method: 提出 FastOMOP 三层架构：1) 治理层通过过程边界的确定性验证强制执行安全控制，独立于智能体推理；2) 可观测层；3) 编排层。智能体团队（表型分析、研究设计、统计分析）通过受控工具暴露继承安全保证。

Result: 在 Synthea、MIMIC-IV 和 NHS IDRIL 三个数据集上，自然语言转 SQL 智能体团队达到 0.84-0.94 可靠性评分，对抗性和越界攻击拦截率完美。

Conclusion: RWE 部署的可靠性差距是架构问题而非模型能力问题，FastOMOP 建立了受治理的渐进式 RWE 自动化架构。

Abstract: The Observational Medical Outcomes Partnership Common Data Model (OMOP CDM), maintained by the Observational Health Data Sciences and Informatics (OHDSI) collaboration, enabled the harmonisation of electronic health records data of nearly one billion patients in 83 countries. Yet generating real-world evidence (RWE) from these repositories remains a manual process requiring clinical, epidemiological and technical expertise. LLMs and multi-agent systems have shown promise for clinical tasks, but RWE automation exposes a fundamental challenge: agentic systems introduce emergent behaviours, coordination failures and safety risks that existing approaches fail to govern. No infrastructure exists to ensure agentic RWE generation is flexible, safe and auditable across the lifecycle. We introduce FastOMOP, an open-source multi-agent architecture that addresses this gap by separating three infrastructure layers, governance, observability and orchestration, from pluggable agent-teams. Governance is enforced at the process boundary through deterministic validation independent of agent reasoning, ensuring no compromised or hallucinating agent can bypass safety controls. Agent teams for phenotyping, study design and statistical analysis inherit these guarantees through controlled tool exposure. We validated FastOMOP using a natural-language-to-SQL agent team across three OMOP CDM datasets: synthetic data from Synthea, MIMIC-IV and a real-world NHS dataset from Lancashire Teaching Hospitals (IDRIL). FastOMOP achieved reliability scores of 0.84-0.94 with perfect adversarial and out-of-scope block rates, demonstrating process-boundary governance delivers safety guarantees independent of model choice. These results indicate that the reliability gap in RWE deployment is architectural rather than model capability, and establish FastOMOP as a governed architecture for progressive RWE automation.

</details>


### [153] [A systematic evaluation of vision-language models for observational astronomical reasoning tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24589)
*Wenke Ren,Hengxiao Guo,Wenwen Zuo,Xiaoman Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AstroVLBench基准测试，评估视觉语言模型在天文学观测数据（5种模态、4100+实例）上的表现。发现模型性能高度依赖模态，物理知识引导比现象学提示更有效，数值表格优于渲染图像，且模型常从现象学线索得出正确预测但提供物理不精确的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）被越来越多地用作科学数据解释的通用工具，但它们在不同模态的真实天文学观测数据上的可靠性尚未得到测试。

Method: 构建AstroVLBench基准，包含4100+专家验证实例，覆盖光学成像、射电干涉、多波段测光、时域光变曲线和光学光谱5个任务。评估6个前沿模型，进行机制消融实验（现象学提示 vs. 物理提示），并比较数值表格与渲染图表的表示方式。

Result: 性能强烈依赖模态；Gemini 3 Pro在任务中表现最一致，但所有模型均显著低于领域专用方法。物理提示比现象学提示效果更好，产生更均衡的分类；数值表格相比渲染图提升高达13个百分点。模型可能从现象学线索得出正确预测但提供物理不精确的推理。

Conclusion: 研究提供了VLM在观测天文学中的首个系统性多模态基准，识别了当前模型在表示、基础推理方面的瓶颈，表明仅靠准确率不足以确保可信的科学部署。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed as general-purpose tools for scientific data interpretation, yet their reliability on real astronomical observations across diverse modalities remains untested. We present AstroVLBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising over 4,100 expert-verified instances across five tasks spanning optical imaging, radio interferometry, multi-wavelength photometry, time-domain light curves, and optical spectroscopy. Evaluating six frontier models, we find that performance is strongly modality-dependent: while one model (Gemini 3 Pro) emerges as the most consistently capable across tasks, task-specific strengths vary, and all models substantially underperform domain-specialized methods. Mechanistic ablations reveal that performance depends not only on directing attention to salient visual features but also on grounding those features in physical knowledge. Phenomenological prompts describing what to look for improve accuracy by sharpening model focus, but physical prompts explaining why those features matter perform better overall and yield more balanced classifications with reduced class-specific bias. Consistent with this picture, presenting the underlying one-dimensional measurements directly as numerical tables instead of rendered plots yields up to 13 percentage points improvement. Reasoning quality analysis further demonstrates that, without explicit physical grounding, models may reach correct predictions from phenomenologically plausible cues while providing physically imprecise justifications, establishing that accuracy alone is insufficient for trustworthy scientific deployment. These findings provide the first systematic, multi-modal baselines for VLMs in observational astronomy and identify the specific representation, grounding, and reasoning bottlenecks where current models fail.

</details>


### [154] [NeSyCat: A Monad-Based Categorical Semantics of the Neurosymbolic ULLER Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24612)
*Daniel Romero Schellhorn,Till Mossakowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于单子的范畴论框架，统一了ULLER中经典、模糊和概率三种语义，实现了新语义的模块化添加和系统间翻译，并展示了在LTN中扩展广义量化的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ULLER最初定义了三种两两独立的语义（经典、模糊、概率），但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解这些语义之间的关系，也难以模块化地添加新语义。

Method: 利用范畴论中的单子（monads）——即函数式编程中建模副作用的结构——将三种看似不同的语义统一到一个框架中，使得新语义可以模块化添加，不同语义之间可以系统翻译。

Result: 实现了基于单子的ULLER模块化实现（Python和Haskell），发布了初始版本；展示了如何通过扩展Giry单子到概率空间，在LTN中为任意（包括无限）域添加广义量化。

Conclusion: 单子框架成功统一了ULLER的多种语义，为神经符号系统提供了更灵活、可扩展的理论基础。

Abstract: ULLER (Unified Language for LEarning and Reasoning) offers a unified first-order logic (FOL) syntax, enabling its knowledge bases to be used directly across a wide range of neurosymbolic systems. The original specification endows this syntax with three pairwise independent semantics: classical, fuzzy, and probabilistic, each accompanied by dedicated semantic rules. We show that these seemingly disparate semantics are all instances of one categorical framework based on monads, the very construct that models side effects in functional programming. This enables the modular addition of new semantics and systematic translations between them. As example, we outline the addition of generalised quantification in Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) to arbitrary (also infinite) domains by extending the Giry monad to probability spaces. In particular, our approach allows a modular implementation of ULLER in Python and Haskell, of which we have published initial versions on GitHub.

</details>


### [155] [XGRAG: A Graph-Native Framework for Explaining KG-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24623)
*Zhuoling Li,Ha Linh Hong Tran Nguyen,Valeria Bladinieres,Maxim Romanovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XGRAG框架，通过图基扰动策略量化知识图谱组件对GraphRAG系统回答的因果贡献，实现可解释性增强，在多个数据集上相比基线RAG-Ex提升了14.81%的解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG系统的推理过程是黑盒的，无法理解结构化知识如何影响最终输出；已有的RAG可解释性方法仅适用于基于文本检索的系统，无法处理图结构中的关系，造成透明度和可信度的关键缺口。

Method: 提出XGRAG框架，采用基于图的扰动策略，通过删除或修改知识图谱中的节点/边来量化每个图组件对模型回答的因果贡献度，从而生成因果可解释的解释。

Result: 在NarrativeQA、FairyTaleQA和TriviaQA数据集上，XGRAG的解释质量相比基线RAG-Ex提升了14.81%（以F1分数衡量生成解释与原始答案的对齐程度）；XGRAG的解释与图的中心性度量呈现强相关性，验证了其捕获图结构的能力。

Conclusion: XGRAG提供了一种可扩展且通用的方法，通过透明、基于图的解释增强了RAG系统的可解释性，推动了可信赖AI的发展。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) extends traditional RAG by using knowledge graphs (KGs) to give large language models (LLMs) a structured, semantically coherent context, yielding more grounded answers. However, GraphRAG reasoning process remains a black-box, limiting our ability to understand how specific pieces of structured knowledge influence the final output. Existing explainability (XAI) methods for RAG systems, designed for text-based retrieval, are limited to interpreting an LLM response through the relational structures among knowledge components, creating a critical gap in transparency and trustworthiness. To address this, we introduce XGRAG, a novel framework that generates causally grounded explanations for GraphRAG systems by employing graph-based perturbation strategies, to quantify the contribution of individual graph components on the model answer. We conduct extensive experiments comparing XGRAG against RAG-Ex, an XAI baseline for standard RAG, and evaluate its robustness across various question types, narrative structures and LLMs. Our results demonstrate a 14.81% improvement in explanation quality over the baseline RAG-Ex across NarrativeQA, FairyTaleQA, and TriviaQA, evaluated by F1-score measuring alignment between generated explanations and original answers. Furthermore, XGRAG explanations exhibit a strong correlation with graph centrality measures, validating its ability to capture graph structure. XGRAG provides a scalable and generalizable approach towards trustworthy AI through transparent, graph-based explanations that enhance the interpretability of RAG systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Reconstructive Authority Model: Runtime Execution Validity Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22898)
*Marcelo Fernandez - TraslaIA*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出重构权威模型（RAM），将执行有效性问题从完整性保证重新定义为覆盖范围重构问题。RAM通过显式推理覆盖范围（包括已证明状态、已声明假设和不可观测残差），在覆盖不足时动态缩小权限或安全失败，从而解决仅靠认证（attestation）无法保证执行有效性的根本缺陷。实验证明RAM在所有覆盖级别上实现了零无效执行率，而纯认证系统即使在完全覆盖下也存在23.3%的无效执行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有治理机制（可信执行环境、预言机签名状态证明、密码学认证）虽然能保证计算和状态投影的完整性，但存在结构性不足：认证的状态投影是执行有效性的必要条件但非充分条件。仅靠完整性检查无法检测未定义状态处理失败等覆盖范围问题，导致即使在完全覆盖下仍存在高无效执行率。

Method: RAM将完整性（integrity）与覆盖范围（coverage）分离，引入重构门（reconstruction gate）：显式推理覆盖范围包络（包含已证明状态、已声明假设和不可观测残差），仅当覆盖范围对动作类足够时才允许执行；覆盖不足时动态缩小权限或安全失败。通过两个定理（认证不足定理和RAM必要性定理）及三个推论进行形式化证明，并提出混合RAM+认证架构。

Result: 合成实验（N=100,000, seed=42）显示：RAM在所有覆盖级别上实现零无效执行率；纯认证系统在低覆盖时无效执行率（IER）为0.423，即使在完全覆盖下IER仍为0.233（源于完整性检查无法检测的未定义状态处理失败）。

Conclusion: 执行有效性应被重新定义为覆盖范围重构问题，与认证提供的完整性保证不同但互补。RAM填补了现有治理机制的结构性缺陷，证明了仅靠完整性保证不足以确保自治系统在部分可观测条件下的安全执行。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly operate under partial observability where execution-relevant state is never fully accessible. Existing governance mechanisms -- trusted execution environments, oracle-signed state proofs, cryptographic attestation -- enforce the integrity of computation and state projections. We show this is structurally insufficient: an authenticated projection of state is necessary but never sufficient for execution validity.
  We introduce the Reconstructive Authority Model (RAM), which separates integrity from coverage. RAM defines a reconstruction gate that reasons over an explicit coverage envelope -- comprising proven state, declared assumptions, and an acknowledged unobservable residual -- and permits execution only when coverage is adequate for the action class. When coverage is insufficient, RAM narrows privileges dynamically or fails closed. Attestation proves trust in measurement; RAM proves adequacy of what is measured.
  We formalize RAM, prove necessity via two theorems (attestation insufficiency and RAM necessity) and three corollaries, and present a hybrid RAM+Attestation architecture with privilege-narrowing. Synthetic experiments (N=100,000, seed=42) show RAM achieves zero invalid execution rates at all coverage levels. Attestation-based systems exhibit IER=0.423 at low coverage and IER=0.233 even at full coverage, the latter arising from undefined-state handling failures undetectable by integrity checks alone.
  This reframes execution validity as a coverage reconstruction problem, distinct from and complementary to integrity guarantees provided by attestation.

</details>


### [157] [Module Lattice Security (Part II): Module Lattice Reduction via Optimal Sign Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22900)
*Ming-Xing Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将CDPR格约简算法从理想格扩展至模格，利用幂基的迹正交性将模分解为秩1子模并独立应用CDPR，在类数为一条件下实现了与理想格情形匹配的Hermite因子$\exp(\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n}))$和与秩无关的模约简因子$O(1)$。通过CRT缩放舍入控制精度，并将符号选择子问题重构为混合整数线性规划，确定最优平衡偏差为通用常数$\delta^*\approx 0.4407$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CDPR算法从理想格推广到模格，这是格密码中更一般的代数结构；同时解决高精度实现中的精度控制和符号选择子问题的最优性。

Method: 利用幂基的迹正交性将模分解为秩1子模，独立应用CDPR；在完全分裂素理想处引入CRT缩放舍入以降低Gram-Schmidt舍入误差；将CDPR符号选择子问题重构为混合整数线性规划确定最优常数。

Result: 模约简的Hermite因子为$\exp(\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n}))$，与理想格情形一致；模约简因子为与秩无关的常数$O(1)$；最优平衡偏差为$\delta^*\approx 0.4407$。

Conclusion: 在类数为一条件下，CDPR算法可有效推广至模格，且精度可控、符号选择最优，为模格上的格约简提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We extend the CDPR lattice reduction algorithm from ideal to module lattices, leveraging the trace orthogonality of the power basis to decompose the module into rank-1 submodules and applying CDPR independently to each. This base module reduction achieves a Hermite factor $\exp(\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n}))$ matching the ideal case, with a module reduction factor $O(1)$ independent of the rank, under a balance hypothesis automatically satisfied for MLWE-distributed bases. To control precision, we introduce CRT-scaled rounding at totally split primes, reducing the Gram-Schmidt rounding error and yielding a bounded-precision implementation. We further reformulate the CDPR sign-selection subproblem as a mixed-integer linear program, determining the optimal balanced discrepancy to be a universal constant $δ^*\approx 0.4407$. All results build on the class number one condition $h_k^+=1$ established in Part I of this series.

</details>


### [158] [Secure eFPGA-Enabled Edge LLM Inference: Architectural and Hardware Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22935)
*Voktho Das,M Zafir Sadik Khan,Jafar Vafaei,Kimia Azar,Hadi Kamali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于ASIC的Transformer模型边缘部署面临的安全威胁（侧信道攻击、故障注入、硬件木马等），并提出了一种混合ASIC+eFPGA架构，利用eFPGA的可重构性实现运行时监控、侧信道缓解和部署后修补，以在不牺牲ASIC性能的前提下增强系统安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASIC加速器在Transformer边缘部署中性能优异但存在严重安全漏洞（侧信道攻击、故障注入、硬件木马等），现有方案难以兼顾安全性与性能。

Method: 提出混合ASIC+eFPGA架构，将ASIC的高效计算与eFPGA的可重构逻辑相结合，利用eFPGA实现自适应运行时监控、侧信道缓解和部署后补丁更新等安全机制。

Result: 该架构在保持ASIC推理性能优势的同时，增强了对运行时攻击和供应链攻击的防御能力。

Conclusion: 混合ASIC+eFPGA架构是一种平衡性能与安全性的有效方案，能够在Transformer边缘部署场景下提供更强的系统韧性。

Abstract: Edge deployment of transformer-based models increasingly relies on ASIC accelerators due to their high performance and energy efficiency, achieved through optimized dataflows, specialized architectures, low-bitwidth computation, and efficient memory hierarchies. However, these advantages come with significant security vulnerabilities. ASIC-based DNN accelerators are susceptible to side-channel attacks (e.g., power, electromagnetic, and timing analysis) and fault injection attacks (e.g., voltage manipulation, clock glitches, and memory perturbations), which can lead to model extraction or compromised inference integrity. Furthermore, threats introduced during design and fabrication, such as hardware Trojans or untrusted third-party IPs, further expand the attack surface. To address these challenges, we explore a hybrid ASIC+eFPGA architecture that combines the efficiency of ASICs with the flexibility of reconfigurable logic. The integrated eFPGA enables security-oriented mechanisms such as adaptive runtime monitoring, side-channel mitigation and post-deployment patching. By leveraging these capabilities, the proposed approach enhances system resilience against both runtime and supply-chain attacks, while preserving the performance benefits of ASIC-based transformer inference.

</details>


### [159] [Self-Supervised Learning for Android Malware Detection on a Time-Stamped Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23025)
*Annan Fu,Hao Pei,Maryam Tanha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Android恶意软件检测中机器学习模型因忽略时间顺序导致的时间偏差问题，构建了带时间戳的数据集，提出基于BYOL自监督预训练和时序感知评估的检测框架，实现98%准确率与89%F1分数，并结合VirusTotal与MITRE ATT&CK框架分析恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Android恶意软件检测器受时间偏差影响严重——模型训练和评估未尊重应用的实际发布时间，导致准确率被高估，实际鲁棒性不足。

Method: 1) 构建带时间戳的良性/恶意Android应用数据集，并设计时间戳验证流程确保时序准确性；2) 采用BYOL（Bootstrap Your Own Latent）进行自监督预训练，学习抗混淆的表征；3) 基于学到的表征进行有监督分类；4) 使用VirusTotal和MITRE ATT&CK框架对真阳性和假阴性进行恶意行为分析。

Result: 在时序感知评估下，该方法达到98%准确率和89%F1分数。

Conclusion: 通过时间感知的评估和BYOL自监督预训练，能有效缓解时间偏差，构建鲁棒的Android恶意软件检测系统，且公开数据集和代码支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Android malware detectors built with machine learning often suffer from temporal bias: models are trained and evaluated without respecting apps' actual release times, inflating accuracy and weakening real-world robustness. We address this by constructing a time-stamped dataset of benign and malicious Android apps and introducing a timestamp-verification procedure to ensure temporal accuracy. We then propose a detection framework that uses Bootstrap Your Own Latent (BYOL) for self-supervised pre-training to learn obfuscation-resilient representations, followed by supervised classification. Under time-aware evaluation, the method attains 98% accuracy and 89% F1. We further characterize malware behavior by analyzing true positives and false negatives using VirusTotal and the MITRE ATT&CK framework. To support reproducibility and further innovation, we release our dataset and source code.

</details>


### [160] [Training a General Purpose Automated Red Teaming Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23067)
*Aishwarya Padmakumar,Leon Derczynski,Traian Rebedea,Christopher Parisien*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需预置评估器、可泛化到任意对抗目标的红队测试模型训练流水线，通过对Qwen3-8B等小模型微调，显著提升其在域内和域外对抗目标上的攻击生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化红队方法主要针对安全与内容审核，依赖内容安全模型作为评估器，未能处理这些模型未捕获的其他对抗意图，且缺乏对未见对抗目标的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种流水线，训练红队模型使其能泛化到任意对抗目标（包括未直接训练的目标），且不依赖训练时存在预置评估器；具体通过对Qwen3-8B等小模型进行微调实现。

Result: 微调后的小模型在域内和域外对抗目标上的攻击生成能力均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效拓展了自动化红队测试的适用范围，使其能处理更广泛的对抗性目标，且不依赖预置评估器。

Abstract: Automated methods for red teaming LLMs are an important tool to identify LLM vulnerabilities that may not be covered in static benchmarks, allowing for more thorough probing. They can also adapt to each specific LLM to discover weaknesses unique to it. Most current automated red teaming methods are intended for tackling safety and content moderation. Thus, they make use of content safety models as evaluators and optimize for circumventing them, and as such, have not been tested with other adversarial intents not typically captured by these. We propose a pipeline for training a red teaming model that can generalize to arbitrary adversarial goals, including objectives it has not been directly trained on, and that does not depend on the existence of a pre-existing evaluator available at training time. We demonstrate that finetuning small models, such as Qwen3-8B, using this pipeline results in a substantial improvement in their ability to generate attacks for both in and out of domain adversarial goals.

</details>


### [161] [From Language to Logic: Bridging LLMs & Formal Representations for RTL Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23100)
*Nowfel Mashnoor,Hadi Kamali,Kimia Azar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ProofLoop，一个基于工具增强型ReAct智能体，通过求解器协同（solver-in-the-loop）方法从自然语言规格自动生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA），在FVEval Design2SVA基准上实现93.7%语法正确率和82.0%功能正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动编写SVA需要深厚的数字硬件设计和时序逻辑专业知识，现有LLM自动化方法存在信号引用错误、时序约束缺失以及缺乏形式化正确性保证等问题。

Method: ProofLoop采用两阶段ReAct智能体架构：阶段A通过语义搜索AST索引向量数据库和JasperGold结构查询自动收集设计上下文；阶段B生成SVA并利用JasperGold形式证明反馈进行最多3轮迭代精炼。

Result: 在FVEval Design2SVA基准上，语法正确率达93.7%，功能正确率达82.0%。消融实验证实RAG、JasperGold工具和验证循环三个组件均独立且正交地贡献性能提升。

Conclusion: ProofLoop通过工具增强与求解器协同的形式化反馈循环，有效提升了LLM生成SVA的正确性和可靠性。

Abstract: SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA) are essential for formal verification of digital hardware, yet their manual creation demands significant expertise in both the design under verification and temporal logic. Recent studies have explored using large language models (LLMs) to automate SVA generation, but existing approaches suffer from incorrect signal references, missing timing constraints, and lack of formal correctness guarantees. This paper presents ProofLoop, a tool-augmented ReAct agent that generates SVA from natural-language specifications using a solver-in-the-loop approach. The agent operates in two phases: Phase A autonomously gathers design context by invoking EDA and formal tools, including semantic search over an AST-indexed vector database and JasperGold structural queries, while Phase B generates SVA and iteratively refines it using JasperGold formal proof feedback over up to fixed (here 3) verification rounds. We evaluate ProofLoop on FVEval Design2SVA design benchmarks and demonstrate that this framework can achieve 93.7% syntax correctness and 82.0% functional correctness. An ablation study confirms that each component, i.e., retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), JasperGold tools, and the verification loop contributes significantly (and orthogonally).

</details>


### [162] [UNSEEN: A Cross-Stack LLM Unlearning Defense against AR-LLM Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23141)
*Tianlong Yu,Yang Yang,Xiao Luo,Lihong Liu,Fudu Xing,Zui Tao,Kailong Wang,Gaoyang Liu,Ting Bi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出UNSEEN，一种针对增强现实与大语言模型结合的新型社会工程攻击（AR-LLM-SE）的跨栈防御框架，通过AR访问控制层、基于F-RMU的LLM遗忘机制和运行时智能体护栏，在60人用户研究和360条标注对话中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-LLM结合的社会工程攻击（如SEAR）对现实社交构成严重威胁，攻击者通过AR眼镜获取目标图像和语音信息，利用LLM识别目标和生成社交画像，再通过LLM智能体应用社会工程策略获取信任并实施钓鱼。现有防御方法（如基于角色的访问控制或数据流追踪）不适用于AR-LLM融合生态系统，因为AR设备资源受限且LLM推理过程不透明，亟需超越仅依赖立法和用户教育的人类中心措施，转向可执行的厂商策略和平台级限制。

Method: 提出UNSEEN跨栈防御框架，包含三层：1) AR ACL（访问控制层）实现基于身份门控的感知控制；2) 基于F-RMU的LLM遗忘机制抑制敏感画像生成；3) 运行时智能体护栏实现自适应交互控制。

Result: 通过IRB批准的60人用户研究和360条跨现实社交场景标注对话进行评估，验证了UNSEEN在防御AR-LLM社会工程攻击方面的有效性。

Conclusion: AR-LLM社会工程攻击是新兴且强大的威胁，现有隐私范式难以应对，UNSEEN通过跨栈协同防御为解决这一挑战提供了可行方案，推动了从人类中心措施向可执行厂商策略和平台级限制的转变。

Abstract: Emerging AR-LLM-based Social Engineering attack (e.g., SEAR) is at the edge of posing great threats to real-world social life. In such AR-LLM-SE attack, the attacker can leverage AR (Augmented Reality) glass to capture the image and vocal information of the target, using the LLM to identify the target and generate the social profile, using the LLM agents to apply social engineering strategies for conversation suggestion to win the target trust and perform phishing afterwards. Current defensive approaches, such as role-based access control or data flow tracking, are not directly applicable to the convergent AR-LLM ecosystem (considering embedded AR device and opaque LLM inference), leaving an emerging and potent social engineering threat that existing privacy paradigms are ill-equipped to address. This necessitates a shift beyond solely human-centric measures like legislation and user education toward enforceable vendor policies and platform-level restrictions. Realizing this vision, however, faces significant technical challenges: securing resource-constrained AR-embedded devices, implementing fine-grained access control within opaque LLM inferences, and governing adaptive interactive agents. To address these challenges, we present UNSEEN, a coordinated cross-stack defense that combines an AR ACL (Access Control Layer) for identity-gated sensing, F-RMU-based LLM unlearning for sensitive profile suppression, and runtime agent guardrails for adaptive interaction control. We evaluate UNSEEN in an IRB-approved user study with 60 participants and a dataset of 360 annotated conversations across realistic social scenarios.

</details>


### [163] [AsmRAG: LLM-Driven Malware Detection by Retrieving Functionally Similar Assembly Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23196)
*ElMouatez Billah Karbab*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AsmRAG是一种基于汇编级检索增强生成的恶意软件检测框架，通过将检测转化为证据检索任务，利用代码专用大语言模型将汇编函数转换为语义嵌入，并采用密度加权锚点选择机制提取恶意逻辑单元，在4万二进制样本上达到96%检测F1值和95%家族归属F1值，且对变体混淆攻击具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习恶意软件检测器虽分类准确率高，但缺乏可解释性，通常只返回概率性判决而不提供取证上下文，无法满足安全运营中心对透明度和可追溯证据的需求。

Method: 提出AsmRAG框架：1) 使用代码专用LLM将汇编函数转换为语义嵌入，构建抗语法混淆的可搜索知识库；2) 提出密度加权锚点选择机制，在二进制文件中隔离主要恶意逻辑单元以提取可验证的取证证据并抵抗逃避攻击。

Result: 在4万二进制样本数据集上，检测F1分数达96%，家族归属F1分数达95%；与EMBER和ResNeXt等全局基线相比，语义检索方法对变体混淆保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AsmRAG为安全运营中心提供了透明可靠的替代方案，当传统全局长短期特征基线性能下降时仍能保持稳健，验证了将检测重构为基于证据的检索任务的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning malware detectors achieve high classification accuracy but suffer from severe interpretability limitations, typically returning probabilistic verdicts that lack forensic context. We introduce AsmRAG, a framework performing malware analysis through Assembly-Level Retrieval-Augmented Generation. Unlike classifiers built on global statistical features, AsmRAG reformulates detection as an evidence-based retrieval task. The system uses a code-specialized Large Language Model (LLM) to analyze assembly functions and convert them into semantic embeddings. This process constructs a searchable knowledge base resilient to syntactic obfuscation. For inference, we propose a Density-Weighted Anchor Selection mechanism that isolates the primary unit of malicious logic within a binary to extract verifiable forensic evidence and resist evasion attempts. Testing on a curated dataset of 40k binaries shows AsmRAG reaching a detection F1-score of 96% alongside a family attribution F1-score of 95%. Comparisons confirm this semantic retrieval approach remains robust against metamorphic obfuscation. When holistic baselines (EMBER and ResNeXt) degrade, our methodology gives Security Operations Centers a transparent and reliable alternative.

</details>


### [164] [Tessera: Secure, Near-Line-Rate Weight Streaming for UMA Edge Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23205)
*Animan Naskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Tessera是一种面向统一内存架构(UMA)边缘加速器的内联缓存行粒度权重解密参考架构，通过并行化AES-256-CTR密钥流计算与DRAM读取，在64字节AXI突发粒度上实现解密，达到98.4%的理论内存带宽上限（仅1.6%开销），相比页级内存加密最高32倍的带宽惩罚，Tessera在所有层几何形状下保持最优1倍内存占用，并能有效抵御物理DRAM提取、恶意DMA和计算劫持等UMA特定攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在UMA系统中，CPU和NPU共享物理DRAM，明文模型权重可直接被受损操作系统内核读取。现有防御方案（可信执行环境、全内存加密）在UMA边缘设备上存在严重问题：TEE永久预留稀缺内存，全内存加密以页粒度操作，导致子系统需要为子页张量块获取巨大4KB内存页，严重损害带宽。

Method: Tessera在64字节AXI总线突发粒度上拦截传输，并行计算AES-256-CTR密钥流与DRAM读取操作，将明文直接流式传输到隔离的NPU SRAM中，创建仅限活动张量块的瞬态内存足迹，消除了永久内存预留的需求。

Result: 在三个不同SoC平台上的测量表明，并行化将密码学延迟隐藏在标准DRAM读取时间之后。Tessera可达到98.4%的理论内存带宽上限（仅1.6%开销）。与页级内存加密高达32倍的带宽惩罚相比，Tessera在所有层几何形状下维持最优1倍内存占用。

Conclusion: Tessera能够有效中和UMA特定的攻击向量（物理DRAM提取、恶意DMA、计算劫持），并形式化地防止稀疏张量上的明文泄露，为UMA边缘设备提供了硬件支持的DRAM级模型保护方案。

Abstract: Deploying proprietary Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) on commodity edge devices demands hardware-backed Digital Rights Management (DRM) capable of withstanding both software-level and physical adversaries. In Unified Memory Architecture (UMA) systems, the host CPU and Neural Processing Unit (NPU) share physical DRAM, leaving plaintext model weights directly readable by a compromised OS kernel. Existing defenses fail in this constrained setting: trusted execution environments monopolize scarce memory with permanently reserved regions, while full-memory encryption operates at page granularity. This forces the system to fetch massive 4 KB memory pages for sub-page tensor tiles, severely crippling bandwidth.
  We present Tessera, a reference architecture for inline, cache-line granularity weight decryption on UMA edge accelerators. The design intercepts 64-byte AXI bursts, computing AES-256-CTR keystreams in parallel with DRAM fetches. This streams plaintext directly into isolated NPU SRAM, creating a transient memory footprint confined to the active tile and eliminating the need for permanent memory carve-outs. Measurements across three distinct SoC platforms demonstrate that this parallelization hides cryptographic latency behind standard DRAM fetch times, a condition that holds even under worst-case timing variations. Consequently, Tessera is projected to achieve 98.4\% of the theoretical memory bandwidth ceiling (a mere 1.6\% overhead). Across standard vision and language models, page-level memory encryption suffers up to a 32x bandwidth penalty, whereas Tessera maintains an optimal 1x footprint for all layer geometries. Finally, Tessera neutralizes major UMA-specific attack vectors -- including physical DRAM extraction, rogue DMA, and compute hijacking -- and formally prevents plaintext leakage across sparse tensors.

</details>


### [165] [Toward Polymorphic Backdoor against Semantic Communication via Intensity-Based Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23231)
*Xiao Yang,Yuni Lai,Gaolei Li,Jun Wu,Kai Zhou,Jianhua Li,Mingzhe Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SemBugger，一种多形态语义通信后门攻击方法，通过动态调整触发强度实现多样化恶意输出，并设计了可证明鲁棒性的防御机制来抵御同质攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信后门采用单一攻击目标的单形态范式，在异构下游场景中攻击多样性、效率和灵活性受限。

Method: 提出SemBugger，通过多效应投毒训练框架实现：1) 分级强度触发器投毒训练数据；2) 分层恶意损失优化SC系统；3) 系统知识根据输入触发强度动态调整产生目标输出。防御方面，通过受控噪声机制添加噪声，并提供理论下界保证。

Result: 在多种SC模型和基准数据集上，SemBugger在保持正常功能的同时实现了高攻击效能，所设计的防御有效中和了SemBugger攻击。

Conclusion: SemBugger突破了单形态后门限制，实现了多形态、细粒度的语义通信后门攻击，同时提出了可证明鲁棒性的防御方法。

Abstract: Semantic Communication (SC) backdoor attacks aim to utilize triggers to manipulate the system into producing predetermined outputs via backdoored shared knowledge. Current SC backdoors adopt monomorphic paradigms with single attack target, which suffers from limited attack diversity, efficiency, and flexibility in heterogeneous downstream scenarios. To overcome the limitations, we propose SemBugger, a polymorphic SC backdoor. By dynamically adjusting the trigger intensity, SemBugger finely-grained controls over the SC knowledge to generate diverse malicious results from the system. Specifically, SemBugger is realized through a multi-effect poisoning-training framework. It introduces graded-intensity triggers to poison training data and optimizes SC systems with hierarchical malicious loss. The trained system's knowledge dynamically adapts to trigger intensity in inputs to yield target outputs, all while preserving transmission fidelity for benign samples. Moreover, to augment SC security, we propose a provable robustness defense that resists SemBugger's homogeneous attacks through a controlled noise mechanism. It operates via strategically adding noise in SC inputs, and we formally provide a theoretical lower bound on the defense efficacy. Experiments across diverse SC models and benchmark datasets indicate that SemBugger attains high attack efficacy while maintaining the regular functionality of SC systems. Meanwhile, the designed defense effectively neutralizes SemBugger attacks.

</details>


### [166] [Training Machine Learning Models on Encrypted Data: A Privacy-Preserving Framework using Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23245)
*Alexandre Marques,Beatriz Sá,Rui Botelho,Pedro Pinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CKKS同态加密的隐私保护机器学习框架，实验证明在加密数据上训练的KNN和线性回归模型性能与明文训练模型相当，同时评估了MLP的加密推理，验证了同态加密在保护数据隐私的同时保持模型可用性的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习依赖敏感数据集进行决策，但传统加密方法无法保护数据处理过程中的隐私安全，因此需要一种能在加密数据上直接计算而不泄露原始信息的解决方案。

Method: 采用Cheon-Kim-Kim-Song (CKKS)方案实现近似实数运算的同态加密，在加密数据上训练K近邻(KNN)和线性回归模型，并对多层感知机(MLP)进行加密推理评估。

Result: 在同态加密下训练的模型获得了与明文训练模型相当的性能指标，验证了该方法在保持模型准确性的同时保护数据隐私的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为隐私保护机器学习在实际应用中的广泛采用奠定了基础，在安全性与计算可行性之间取得了平衡，但仍面临计算开销、噪声管理和非多项式运算支持有限等挑战。

Abstract: The use of Machine Learning (ML) for data-driven decision-making often relies on access to sensitive datasets, which introduces privacy challenges. Traditional encryption methods protect data at rest or in transit but fail to secure it during processing, exposing it to unauthorized access. Homomorphic encryption emerges as a transformative solution, enabling computations on encrypted data without decryption, thus preserving confidentiality throughout the ML pipeline. This paper addresses the challenge of training ML models on encrypted data while maintaining accuracy and efficiency by proposing a proof-of-concept for a privacy-preserving framework that leverages Cheon-Kim-Kim-Song (CKKS) for approximate real-number arithmetic. Also, it demonstrates the feasibility of training K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN) and linear regression models on encrypted data, and evaluates encrypted inference for a basic Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) architecture. Experimental results show that models trained under Homomorphic encryption achieve performance metrics comparable to plaintext-trained models, validating the approach. However, challenges such as computational overhead, noise management, and limited support for non-polynomial operations persist. This work lays the groundwork for broader adoption of privacy-preserving ML in real-world applications, balancing security with computational feasibility.

</details>


### [167] [PrivacyAssist: A User-Centric Agent Framework for Detecting Privacy Inconsistencies in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23248)
*Tran Thanh Lam Nguyen,Edoardo Di Tullio,Barbara Carminati,Elena Ferrari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivacyAssist是一个基于多智能体LLM的平台，通过RAG技术检测用户授予的权限与开发者声明的数据收集/共享实践之间的不一致性，提供实时设备警告。对2347个Android应用的分析发现仅16%的应用权限与声明完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用隐私保护措施对用户而言既低效又令人困惑，现有工作主要分析隐私漏洞，但很少帮助用户理解、设置和执行隐私偏好。

Method: 提出PrivacyAssist，一个多智能体LLM平台，利用检索增强生成（RAG）技术，检测用户授予的权限与开发者声明的敏感数据收集和共享实践之间的不一致性，提供简洁解释和实时设备端警告。

Result: 在200名用户和2347个Android应用上评估，发现仅16%的应用在授予权限与声明的数据实践之间完全一致。

Conclusion: PrivacyAssist能有效帮助用户识别权限与数据实践之间的不一致性，支持用户在安装时做出更明智的决策。

Abstract: Mobile apps offer significant benefits, but their privacy protections often remain ineffective and confusing for users. While prior work mainly analyzes app privacy vulnerabilities, few approaches help users understand, set, and enforce their privacy preferences. This paper presents PrivacyAssist, a multi-agent LLM-based platform that detects inconsistencies between user-granted permissions and developers' declared sensitive data collection and sharing practices. Using Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), PrivacyAssist provides concise explanations and real-time on-device warnings to support informed installation decisions. We evaluate PrivacyAssist with 200 users and 2,347 Android apps, finding that only 16% of apps are fully consistent between granted permissions and declared data practices.

</details>


### [168] [Branch Landing: Bloom Filter-Based Source Authorization for Forward-Edge CFI on RISC-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23331)
*You Wu,Peter Beerel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Branch Landing (BRL)，一种基于 Bloom 过滤器查询的前向边 CFI 框架，通过两个轻量级 RISC-V ISA 扩展（bld 和 brl）实现源域授权验证，在 81 个 BEEBS 基准测试中仅引入 0.210%–0.421% 的平均运行时开销和约 0.5% 的代码大小增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向边 CFI 机制缺乏精确的源域授权：基于类型的 CFI 允许所有同签名的调用者，而基于标签的硬件 CFI 受限于固定宽度的寄存器存储，无法灵活支持任意数量的已授权源。

Method: 用 Bloom 过滤器成员查询替代固定容量的授权检查，设计 bld（传播源段标识符 SID 到专用 BRState 寄存器）和 brl（在着陆点执行 Bloom 过滤器验证）两条 RISC-V ISA 扩展；支持从类型级到 CFG 派生级的可配置段粒度授权策略。

Result: 在 81 个 BEEBS 基准测试中，函数级类型策略和基本块级 CFG 派生策略分别仅引入 0.210% 和 0.421% 的平均运行时开销，代码大小增长 0.46% 和 0.52%；CFG 派生策略相比类型策略平均等价类大小降低 32.5%，所有执行均无 BRL 验证失败。

Conclusion: BRL 通过 Bloom 过滤器的可配置设计，在低开销下实现了比类型级 CFI 更精细的源域授权，为 RISC-V 硬件辅助 CFI 提供了灵活且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Jump-Oriented Programming (JOP) attacks exploit indirect control transfers to bypass backward-edge defenses, yet existing forward-edge CFI mechanisms lack precise source-domain authorization: type-based CFI admits all same-signature callers, while tag-based hardware CFI is limited by fixed-width register storage that caps the number of simultaneously authorized sources. We propose Branch Landing (BRL), a landing-based forward-edge CFI framework for RISC-V that replaces fixed-capacity checks with Bloom filter membership queries. Two lightweight ISA extensions, bld and brl, propagate a source Section Identifier (SID) through a dedicated BRState register and validate it at each landing site with fixed-probe latency that is independent of the number of authorized sources under a chosen filter configuration. Section granularity is configurable, supporting policies from type-based to CFG-derived authorization within a single mechanism. We implement Branch Landing in the LLVM RISC-V backend and evaluate it on 81 BEEBS benchmarks under two representative policy configurations: a function-level, type-based policy and a basic-block-level, CFG-derived policy. Under a 3-cycle brl latency model, the two configurations incur average runtime overheads of only 0.210% and 0.421%, with mean code size growth of 0.46% and 0.52% respectively. The CFG-derived policy reduces the average equivalence class size by 32.5% compared to the type-based policy, and all evaluated executions complete without BRL enforcement failures.

</details>


### [169] [Evaluating Jailbreaking Vulnerabilities in LLMs Deployed as Assistants for Smart Grid Operations: A Benchmark Against NERC Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23341)
*Taha Hammadia,Lucas Rea,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Amr Youssef,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种主流LLM（GPT-4o mini、Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite、Claude 3.5 Haiku）在电力系统场景下面对提示词越狱攻击的脆弱性，基于NERC可靠性标准设计了攻击实验，发现DeepInception方法最有效（ASR 63.17%），Claude 3.5 Haiku完全免疫，而Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite最易受攻击（ASR 55.04%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM作为电网运营助手虽能提升合规与决策效率，但也引入了基于提示词的对抗攻击新风险，特别是授权用户（如操作员）可能通过精心构造的恶意提示诱导模型输出违规内容，因此需要系统评估LLM在此关键基础设施场景下的安全鲁棒性。

Method: 选取OpenAI GPT-4o mini、Google Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite、Anthropic Claude 3.5 Haiku三种LLM，基于NERC九项可靠性标准（EOP、TOP、CIP系列）构建电网场景，使用Baseline、BitBypass、DeepInception三种越狱攻击方法，进行两轮实验：第一轮全面测试，第二轮优化Baseline和BitBypass的恶意措辞后复测。

Result: 整体ASR为33.1%；DeepInception攻击效果最强（ASR 63.17%）；Claude 3.5 Haiku完全抵抗（ASR 0%）；Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite最脆弱（ASR 55.04%）；GPT-4o mini中等（ASR 44.34%）；优化措辞后Baseline和BitBypass攻击的ASR为30.6%，证明简单方法的有效性可通过措辞微调提升。

Conclusion: LLM在电网等关键基础设施场景中存在显著的越狱攻击风险，不同模型安全防护能力差异巨大；攻击方法的复杂度并非决定成败的唯一因素，提示措辞的精细调整也能显著提升简单攻击方法的成功率；需要对LLM在关键基础设施中的应用建立更严格的安全评估与防护机制。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) as assistants in electric grid operations promises to streamline compliance and decision-making but exposes new vulnerabilities to prompt-based adversarial attacks. This paper evaluates the risk of jailbreaking LLMs, i.e., circumventing safety alignments to produce outputs violating regulatory standards, assuming threats from authorized users, such as operators, who craft malicious prompts to elicit non-compliant guidance. Three state-of-the-art LLMs (OpenAI's GPT-4o mini, Google's Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite, and Anthropic's Claude 3.5 Haiku) were tested against Baseline, BitBypass, and DeepInception jailbreaking methods across scenarios derived from nine NERC Reliability Standards (EOP, TOP, and CIP). In the initial broad experiment, the overall Attack Success Rate (ASR) was 33.1%, with DeepInception proving most effective at 63.17% ASR. Claude 3.5 Haiku exhibited complete resistance (0% ASR), while Gemini 2.0 Flash-Lite was most vulnerable (55.04% ASR) and GPT-4o mini moderately susceptible (44.34% ASR). A follow-up experiment refining malicious wording in Baseline and BitBypass attacks yielded a 30.6% ASR, confirming that subtle prompt adjustments can enhance simpler methods' efficacy.

</details>


### [170] [Ghost in the Agent: Redefining Information Flow Tracking for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23374)
*Yuandao Cai,Wensheng Tang,Cheng Wen,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NeuroTaint是首个为LLM智能体量身定制的污点追踪框架，通过语义变换、因果推理和跨会话持久性上下文追踪，在离线状态下审计执行轨迹，有效检测从不可信来源到特权接收器的信息流传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在与外部工具、API和内存存储交互时面临间接提示注入和未授权工具执行等严重安全威胁，而传统基于程序内存状态的污点分析不适用于由概率性自然语言推理驱动的LLM智能体。

Method: 提出NeuroTaint框架，将LLM智能体中的污点传播理解为显式内容传输、语义变换、因果决策影响和跨会话持久性四个方面，通过语义证据、因果推理和持久化上下文追踪离线审计执行轨迹，重构从不可信源到特权接收器的传播路径。

Result: 基于包含400个场景的TaintBench基准测试（覆盖20个真实世界智能体框架），NeuroTaint在源-接收器传播检测上显著优于FIDES基线，并在InjecAgent和ToolEmu等现有智能体安全基准上保持有效性，且离线运行的开销有限。

Conclusion: NeuroTaint为LLM智能体提供了有效的污点追踪方案，弥补了传统方法在语义级信息流追踪上的不足，对保障LLM智能体安全具有重要价值。

Abstract: Autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed to conduct complex tasks by interacting with external tools, APIs, and memory stores. However, processing untrusted external data exposes these agents to severe security threats, such as indirect prompt injection and unauthorized tool execution. Securing these systems requires effective information flow tracking. Yet, traditional taint analysis that is designed for program memory states fundamentally fails when applied to LLMs, where data propagation is governed by probabilistic natural language reasoning. In this paper, we present NeuroTaint, the first comprehensive taint tracking framework tailored for the unique information flow characteristics of LLM agents. Our key insight is that taint propagation in LLM agents must be understood not only as explicit content transfer, but also as semantic transformation, causal influence on decisions, and cross-session persistence through memory. NeuroTaint therefore audits execution traces offline to reconstruct provenance from untrusted sources to privileged sinks using semantic evidence, causal reasoning, and persistent context tracking, rather than relying on exact string matches or pre-defined source-sink paths alone. Extensive evaluation using TaintBench, our 400-scenario benchmark spanning 20 real-world agent frameworks, shows that NeuroTaint substantially outperforms FIDES, an information-flow-control (IFC)-style baseline for LLM agents, in source-sink propagation detection. We further show that NeuroTaint remains effective on established agent-security benchmarks, including InjecAgent and ToolEmu, while operating offline with modest additional auditing cost.

</details>


### [171] [Scalable and Verifiable Federated Learning for Cross-Institution Financial Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23437)
*Prajwal Panth,Nishant Nigam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出动态分片联邦学习（DSFL），通过随机分片、线性完整性标签和主动邻居恢复协议，在跨机构金融欺诈检测中实现线性可扩展、隐私保护且鲁棒的聚合，相比Paillier方案延迟降低约33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议在可扩展性、隐私性和完整性三者之间存在权衡：同态加密计算成本高，配对掩码协议需O(N²)密钥交换且缺乏异常更新检测机制，同时梯度反转攻击可重建敏感交易数据。

Method: DSFL包含三项核心设计：(1) 动态随机分片将通信复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N·m)；(2) 线性完整性标签作为加性同态承诺机制，实现无需零知识证明的概率性更新验证；(3) 主动邻居恢复协议处理参与者掉线情况。

Result: 在信用卡欺诈检测数据集上，DSFL相比Paillier安全聚合延迟降低约33倍，并在模拟故障下保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSFL为可扩展、隐私保护的跨机构协作欺诈检测提供了实用基础。

Abstract: The global financial ecosystem confronts a critical asymmetry: while fraud syndicates operate as borderless, distributed networks, banking institutions remain constrained by regulatory data silos, limiting visibility into cross-institutional threat patterns under strict privacy laws such as GDPR. Although Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training, existing protocols impose a trade-off among scalability, privacy, and integrity. Homomorphic encryption schemes are computationally expensive, while pairwise masking protocols require O(N^2) key exchanges and lack mechanisms to detect malformed updates. Existing defenses also remain vulnerable to gradient inversion attacks that can reconstruct sensitive transaction data.
  To address these limitations, we propose Dynamic Sharded Federated Learning (DSFL), a verifiable secure aggregation framework for cross-institution financial fraud detection. DSFL replaces mesh topologies with Dynamic Stochastic Sharding, reducing communication complexity from O(N^2) to O(N m), where m is a fixed shard size, achieving linear scalability. To mitigate insider threats, we introduce Linear Integrity Tags, an additive-homomorphic commitment mechanism that enables probabilistic verification of submitted updates without the overhead of zero-knowledge proofs, while not enforcing semantic correctness. Additionally, the Active Neighborhood Recovery protocol ensures robust aggregation under participant dropouts. Empirical evaluation on the Credit Card Fraud Detection Dataset (ULB) demonstrates an approximately 33x latency reduction compared to Paillier-based secure aggregation, while maintaining strong resilience under simulated failures. These results position DSFL as a practical foundation for scalable and privacy-preserving collaborative fraud detection.

</details>


### [172] [ARIstoteles -- Dissecting Apple's Baseband Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23457)
*Tobias Kröll,Stephan Kleber,Frank Kargl,Matthias Hollick,Jiska Classen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次逆向工程并模糊测试了iOS设备上的Apple Remote Invocation (ARI)协议，开发了名为ARIstoteles的Wireshark解析器，发现该接口存在安全缺陷且未得到充分测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前蜂窝基带安全研究多集中于Android平台，苹果设备存在巨大空白，且iOS上的无线芯片接口协议（ARI）缺乏文档和安全研究。

Method: 通过逆向工程iOS闭源库，利用Ghidra脚本自动生成Wireshark解析器ARIstoteles，并基于逆向结果对ARI接口进行模糊测试。

Result: 成功开发了ARIstoteles解析器，模糊测试结果显示ARI接口缺乏安全研究且未被Apple充分测试。

Conclusion: ARI接口存在安全缺陷，开源ARIstoteles有助于推动未来相关安全研究。

Abstract: Wireless chips and interfaces expose a substantial remote attack surface. As of today, most cellular baseband security research is performed on the Android ecosystem, leaving a huge gap on Apple devices. With iOS jailbreaks, last-generation wireless chips become fairly accessible for performance and security research. Yet, iPhones were never intended to be used as a research platform, and chips and interfaces are undocumented. One protocol to interface with such chips is Apple Remote Invocation (ARI), which interacts with the central phone component CommCenter and multiple user-space daemons, thereby posing a Remote Code Execution (RCE) attack surface. We are the first to reverse-engineer and fuzz-test the ARI interface on iOS. Our Ghidra scripts automatically generate a Wireshark dissector, called ARIstoteles, by parsing closed-source iOS libraries for this undocumented protocol. Moreover, we compare the quality of the dissector to fully-automated approaches based on static trace analysis. Finally, we fuzz the ARI interface based on our reverse-engineering results. The fuzzing results indicate that ARI does not only lack public security research but also has not been well-tested by Apple. By releasing ARIstoteles open-source, we also aim to facilitate similar research in the future.

</details>


### [173] [Breaking the Secret: Economic Interventions for Combating Collusion in Embodied Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23511)
*Qi Liu,Xiaohui Chen,Zhihui Zhao,Yaowen Zheng,Dan Yu,Zehua Zhang,Limin Sun,Yongle Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诱变激励干预方法，通过重塑智能体的收益结构来抑制具身多智能体系统中的合谋行为，实验证明该方法能有效诱导背叛、抑制合谋，同时保持系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身多智能体系统中，自主智能体之间的合谋行为构成严重安全威胁，而现有防御方法（基于身份控制或事后行为分析）在具身环境下因反馈延迟和观测噪声而难以准确及时地检测行为偏差。

Method: 提出诱变激励干预方法，通过奖励举报合谋行为的智能体并惩罚被识别的参与者来重塑收益结构，使合谋变得不稳定；同时设计了包括举报押金、智能合约奖励执行和加密通信在内的支持机制，以抵御激励机制的滥用和被惩罚智能体的报复。

Result: 在模拟和真实具身环境中的实验表明，该方法通过诱导背叛有效抑制了合谋，性能接近无合谋基线，优于代表性被动防御方法，实现了预期安全目标。

Conclusion: 主动激励设计是保障具身多智能体系统安全的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Collusion among autonomous agents poses a critical security threat in embodied multi-agent systems (MAS), where coordinated behaviors can deviate from global objectives and lead to real-world consequences. Existing defenses, primarily based on identity control or post-hoc behavior analysis, are insufficient to address such threats in embodied settings due to delayed feedback and noisy observations in physical environments, which make behavioral deviations difficult to detect accurately and in a timely manner. To address this challenge, we propose a mutagenic incentive intervention approach that mitigates collusion by reshaping agents' payoff structures. By rewarding agents who report collusive behavior and penalizing identified participants, the mechanism induces strategic defection and renders collusion unstable. We further design supporting mechanisms, including reporting deposits, smart contract-based reward enforcement, and encrypted communication, to ensure robustness against misuse of the incentive mechanism and retaliation from penalized agents. We implement the proposed approach in both simulated and real-world embodied environments. Experimental results show that our method effectively suppresses collusion by inducing defection, while preserving system efficiency. It achieves performance comparable to the non-collusion baseline and outperforms representative reactive defenses, thereby fulfilling the desired security objectives. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of proactive incentive design as a practical paradigm for securing embodied multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [174] [Time-Delayed Publicly Verifiable Quantum Computation for Classical Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23516)
*Ameer Mohammed,Aydin Abadi,Jaffer Mahdi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的、基于时间锁谜题的非交互式公开可验证量子计算委托方案，通过将2轮私有验证方案编译为带时间戳的公开验证方案，实现了在标准后量子假设下的安全性，并在量子随机预言机模型和CRS模型下证明了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典公开可验证委托问题已被广泛研究，但当经典用户委托量子电路给单一量子服务器时，现有方案需使用不切实际或非标准的密码学工具（如不可区分混淆）。本文旨在放松时间要求，提出一种实用的基于标准后量子假设的公开可验证方案。

Method: 利用承诺方案和时间锁谜题，将2轮私有可验证方案编译为非交互式公开可验证方案，并带有时间戳证明。计算任务（量子计算）和谜题求解均外包给服务器。安全性在量子随机预言机模型和公共参考字符串模型下证明。

Result: 提出了一种非交互式、带时间延迟的公开可验证量子计算委托方案，该方案基于标准后量子密码学假设（如时间锁谜题和承诺方案），避免了不可区分混淆等非标准工具，实现了实用化。

Conclusion: 通过放松验证密钥的公开时机（延迟公开），结合时间锁谜题和承诺方案，成功构建了首个基于标准后量子假设的实用非交互式公开可验证量子计算委托协议，为量子计算的外包验证提供了可行的密码学解决方案。

Abstract: Publicly verifiable delegation is a well-known problem involving a user who wishes to outsource a resource-intensive computational task to a more powerful but potentially untrusted server such that any other party is able to efficiently check the veracity of the computation's result. This problem has been extensively studied in the classical domain where the user and server are both non-quantum machines. However, the problem becomes more challenging when the classical user wants to delegate a quantum circuit to a single prover with quantum-computing capabilities. Previous solutions have resorted to using impractical or non-standard cryptographic solutions (e.g. indistinguishability obfuscation) to achieve this requirement. In this work, we relax the requirement to have time-delayed publicly verifiable proofs, where the verification key is made known to the public only when the computation (and its proof) are guaranteed to have been completed. We propose a practical non-interactive scheme leveraging commitment schemes and time-lock puzzles, which can be efficiently realized through well-established and standard post-quantum assumptions. The main idea of our technique lies in using time-lock puzzles to compile a 2-round privately verifiable scheme into a non-interactive publicly verifiable scheme with timestamped proofs, outsourcing not only the quantum computation but the puzzle solving as well. Security is proven in the quantum random oracle model with a common reference string (CRS).

</details>


### [175] [CyberCane: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Privacy-Preserving Phishing Detection with Formal Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23563)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Aniqa Afzal,Qi Zhao,Vigna Majmundar,Pawel Sloboda,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberCane是一个结合符号分析与隐私保护RAG的神经符号学钓鱼检测框架，通过双阶段流水线和形式化本体PhishOnt实现高精度、低误报率且合规的检测，在AI生成攻击上召回率提升78.6个百分点，精度超98%，误报率低至0.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私关键领域需要同时满足近乎零误报、可解释、合规（不向外部API暴露敏感数据）以及对AI生成攻击鲁棒性的钓鱼检测系统，而现有基于规则的系统对新攻击脆弱，LLM检测器则因传输未脱敏数据违反隐私法规。

Method: 采用神经符号学双阶段流水线：第一阶段对邮件元数据应用轻量级符号规则；第二阶段通过RAG进行语义分类，包含自动敏感数据脱敏和仅从钓鱼语料库检索。同时提出PhishOnt OWL本体，通过形式化推理链实现可验证的攻击分类。

Result: 在DataPhish2025（12.3k邮件，混合人类/LLM生成）和Nazario/SpamAssassin数据集上，相较纯符号检测在AI生成威胁上召回率提升78.6个百分点，精度超过98%，误报率低至0.16%。医疗部署场景预估投资回报率542倍。

Conclusion: CyberCane有效解决了隐私关键领域钓鱼检测的精确性、可解释性、合规性和鲁棒性需求，支持可调操作点以适应不同风险容忍度，并提供开源实现。

Abstract: Privacy-critical domains require phishing detection systems that satisfy contradictory constraints: near-zero false positives to prevent workflow disruption, transparent explanations for non-expert staff, strict regulatory compliance prohibiting sensitive data exposure to external APIs, and robustness against AI-generated attacks. Existing rule-based systems are brittle to novel campaigns, while LLM-based detectors violate privacy regulations through unredacted data transmission. We introduce CyberCane, a neuro-symbolic framework integrating deterministic symbolic analysis with privacy-preserving retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Our dual-phase pipeline applies lightweight symbolic rules to email metadata, then escalates borderline cases to semantic classification via RAG with automated sensitive data redaction and retrieval from a phishing-only corpus. We further introduce PhishOnt, an OWL ontology enabling verifiable attack classification through formal reasoning chains. Evaluation on DataPhish2025 (12.3k emails; mixed human/LLM) and Nazario/SpamAssassin demonstrates a 78.6-point recall gain over symbolic-only detection on AI-generated threats, with precision exceeding 98% and FPR as low as 0.16%. Healthcare deployment projects a 542x ROI; tunable operating points support diverse risk tolerances, with open-source implementation at https://github.com/sbhakim/Cybercane.

</details>


### [176] [The Vehicle May Be Sick: Denial of Diagnostic Services by Exploiting the CAN Transport Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23617)
*Seungjin Baek,Seonghoon Jeong,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对ISO 15765-2标准（UDS over CAN的传输层协议）发现了八种新型攻击场景，通过操纵流控制、序列号违规和错误处理滥用等机制，在真实车辆上验证了其中三种攻击可成功导致诊断服务拒绝，造成故障隐藏和传感器读数被篡改等异常诊断结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDS协议广泛应用于车辆诊断，但其底层传输协议ISO 15765-2在设计之初未考虑安全性，存在被攻击的潜在风险，而现有研究对这一传输层的安全漏洞关注不足。

Method: 作者识别了八种利用ISO 15765-2传输层机制的新型攻击场景，包括流控制操纵、序列号违规和错误处理滥用，并在真实乘用车上使用两种不同的诊断工具进行实验验证。

Result: 三种攻击场景成功实现了诊断服务拒绝，导致故障码被隐藏和传感器读数被篡改等异常诊断结果。

Conclusion: ISO 15765-2传输层存在严重安全漏洞，可被利用来欺骗技术人员和驾驶员，使车辆面临显著安全风险。

Abstract: Vehicle diagnostics has become essential for detecting in-vehicle errors and ensuring safety. While the Unified Diagnostic Services (UDS) protocol is widely adopted for diagnostic operations, it relies on the ISO 15765-2 standard as the transport protocol over the Controller Area Network (CAN), which was designed without inherent security considerations. In this paper, we identify eight novel attack scenarios that exploit specific transport layer mechanisms in the ISO 15765-2 standard, including Flow Control manipulation, Sequence Number violations, and error handling abuses. We evaluate these attacks on a real passenger vehicle using two distinct diagnostic tools to demonstrate their practical impact. Our results confirm that three of these attack scenarios successfully induce denial of diagnostic services, leading to abnormal diagnostic results such as concealed faults and manipulated sensor readings. These findings highlight critical vulnerabilities that can deceive technicians and drivers, potentially exposing vehicles to significant safety risks.

</details>


### [177] [Rényi Pufferfish Privacy with Gaussian-based Priors: From Single Gaussian to Mixture Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23649)
*Wenjin Yang,Ni Ding,Zijian Zhang,Zhen Li,Jing Sun,Jincheng An,Yong Liu,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rényi Pufferfish隐私（RPP）框架下的高斯机制，针对高斯和高斯混合先验分布，推导了精确的Rényi散度、松弛的闭式充分条件及噪声单调性，并通过最优传输理论处理非高斯/多模态先验，实验表明所提机制相比基线平均降低48.9%的噪声，显著改善了隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RPP框架下的∞-Wasserstein机制过于保守，导致数据效用损失严重，需要更高效的机制来改善隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 1) 针对单高斯先验，推导高斯扰动后的精确Rényi散度，得到松弛的闭式(α,ε)-RPP充分条件，并刻画噪声校准相对于隐私预算ε和Rényi阶数α的单调性；2) 针对非高斯/多模态先验，用高斯混合模型近似秘密条件输出，引入基于最优传输的RPP充分条件。

Result: 在三个UCI数据集上（统计查询RAW、MEAN和模型输出查询BNN、GP），所提先验感知机制相比基线平均噪声降低48.9%。

Conclusion: 所提高斯机制能有效改善RPP框架下的隐私-效用权衡，显著降低所需噪声量。

Abstract: Rényi Pufferfish Privacy (RPP) provides a Rényi divergence-based privacy framework for correlated data, but existing $\infty$-Wasserstein mechanisms are often conservative and sacrifice data utility. We study Gaussian mechanisms for RPP under Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture priors. For single Gaussian priors, we derive the exact Rényi divergence after Gaussian perturbation, obtain a relaxed closed-form sufficient condition for $(α,ε)$-RPP, and characterize the monotonicity of the calibrated noise with respect to the privacy budget $ε$ and the Rényi order $α$. To handle more general non-Gaussian and multimodal priors, we approximate secret-conditioned outputs with Gaussian mixture models and introduce an optimal-transport-based sufficient condition for RPP. Experiments on three UCI datasets with statistical (\textsc{RAW}, \textsc{MEAN}) and model-output (\textsc{BNN}, \textsc{GP}) queries show that our prior-aware mechanisms consistently require less noise than a recent RPP additive-noise baseline, achieving an average noise reduction of 48.9\%. These results show that our mechanisms can substantially improve the privacy-utility trade-off under RPP.

</details>


### [178] [LLM-CEG: Extending the Classification Error Gauge Framework for Privacy Auditing of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23795)
*Kato Mivule*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM-CEG框架，将原本用于表格数据的分类错误度量（x-CEG）扩展到大型语言模型的隐私审计中，通过成员推理攻击成功率和模型困惑度分别作为隐私和效用度量，迭代调整差分隐私参数。实验表明DP-SGD可将MIA攻击者优势降低71.5%，同时提升分布外效用47-50%，并进一步提出了LLM-SIED工程框架以支持可审计、符合监管的LLM部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原本针对表格数据集的分类错误度量（x-CEG）框架扩展到大型语言模型的隐私审计中，以系统量化LLM的隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 提出LLM-CEG框架，采用成员推理攻击（MIA）成功率作为经验隐私度量，模型困惑度作为效用度量，通过迭代调整差分隐私参数直至两个阈值同时满足。在合成临床PII数据集上使用DP-SGD对DistilGPT-2进行微调，在四种隐私机制下进行验证，并进一步将SIED工程框架扩展到LLM上下文形成LLM-SIED。

Result: DP-SGD将MIA攻击者优势降低71.5%，同时将分布外效用相对过拟合基线提升47-50%。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在窄微调条件下可充当隐式正则化器，LLM-SIED框架提供了符合监管要求的可审计LLM部署流程。

Abstract: This paper extends the Classification Error Gauge (x-CEG) framework, originally developed for measuring the privacy-utility trade-off in tabular datasets, to privacy auditing of Large Language Models (LLMs). We propose LLM-CEG, a systematic framework that employs membership inference attack (MIA) success rates as an empirical privacy gauge and model perplexity as a utility gauge, iteratively adjusting differential privacy parameters until both thresholds are jointly satisfied. A proof-of-concept prototype fine-tunes DistilGPT-2 on a synthetic clinical PII dataset under four privacy regimes using DP-SGD. Results indicate that DP-SGD reduces MIA attacker advantage by 71.5% while simultaneously improving out-of-distribution utility by 47-50% relative to the overfitted baseline, suggesting that differential privacy may act as implicit regularization under narrow fine-tuning conditions. We further extend the SIED engineering framework to the LLM context as LLM-SIED, providing an auditable, regulator-aligned process for privacy-compliant LLM deployment.

</details>


### [179] [SeqShield: A Behavioral Analysis Approach to Uncover Rootkits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23812)
*Paras Ghodeshwar,Sandeep K Shukla,Anand Handa,Nitesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SeqShield，一种基于API调用序列行为分析的Windows Rootkit检测方法。通过n-gram特征提取和随机森林分类器，结合基尼不纯度特征选择优化，实现对变异Rootkits的高效检测，最高准确率达97.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析方法和基于签名的检测技术难以应对具有变形行为的Rootkit恶意软件，需要转向动态分析方法。

Method: 采用API调用序列进行动态行为分析；使用变形代码引擎生成10倍变异Rootkit样本；应用n-gram分析（bigram和trigram）提取特征；使用随机森林分类器检测；利用基尼不纯度指数进行特征重要性排序，迭代选择最优特征以降低维度。

Result: 全特征下随机森林准确率：bigram 97.27%、trigram 96.17%；优化特征后准确率：bigram 96.72%、trigram 97.81%，检测效率显著提升且精度未损失。

Conclusion: 基于API调用序列的行为分析与n-gram特征工程相结合，配合基尼不纯度特征选择优化，能够在降低特征维度的同时保持甚至提升检测准确率，为Rootkit检测提供了一种高效的动态分析方案。

Abstract: Rootkits are among the most elusive types of malware, capable of bypassing traditional static analysis methods due to their metamorphic behavior. Signature-based detection techniques struggle against these threats, necessitating a shift toward dynamic analysis approaches. We propose SeqShield, a behavior-based rootkit detection approach designed specifically for the Windows OS, leveraging API call sequences for dynamic behavior analysis. Instead of relying on static signatures, SeqShield examines the execution patterns of API calls, which inherently reflect malicious intent. Analyzing API sequences, we can effectively identify rootkit-like behavior. We also employed a metamorphic code engine to generate 10X mutated variants of rootkits, demonstrating their obfuscation strategies. SeqShield applies n-gram analysis to extract bigram and trigram features from these API call sequences, enabling effective detection of rootkit-like activity. Among the models tested, Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy of 97.27% (bigram) and 96.17% (trigram). To optimize performance and decrease the dimension, we apply feature importance ranking using the Gini Impurity Index, iteratively selecting the most significant features. The optimized lower-dimensional feature matrix significantly enhances detection efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. Using the optimized feature set, our approach achieves 96.72% accuracy for bigrams and 97.81% accuracy for trigrams.

</details>


### [180] [Evaluation of Prompt Injection Defenses in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23887)
*Priyal Deep,Shane Emmons,Amy Fox,Kyle Bacon,Kelley McAllister,Krisztian Flautner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对LLM系统提示词中嵌入的秘密信息，自适应攻击者在超过20,000次攻击中测试了九种防御配置。所有依赖模型自我保护的防御最终都被攻破，唯一有效的防御是在独立应用代码中通过硬编码规则进行输出过滤，在15,000次攻击中实现零泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM应用将秘密嵌入系统提示词的安全风险，验证现有防御机制的有效性，证明安全边界必须由外部应用代码而非模型本身来强制执行。

Method: 构建自适应攻击者，在数百轮攻击中不断进化策略；对九种防御配置进行超过20,000次攻击测试，对比模型自我保护型防御与输出过滤型防御的效果。

Result: 所有依赖模型自我保护的防御最终都被攻破；输出过滤（在独立应用代码中通过硬编码规则检查模型响应）在15,000次攻击中实现零泄露。

Conclusion: 安全边界必须在应用代码中强制执行，而非由被攻击的模型自身负责。在通过Swept AI等工具验证此类防御之前，处理敏感操作的AI系统应仅限于内部可信人员使用。

Abstract: LLM-powered applications routinely embed secrets in system prompts, yet models can be tricked into revealing them. We built an adaptive attacker that evolves its strategies over hundreds of rounds and tested it against nine defense configurations across more than 20,000 attacks. Every defense that relied on the model to protect itself eventually broke. The only defense that held was output filtering, which checks the model's responses via hardcoded rules in separate application code before they reach the user, achieving zero leaks across 15,000 attacks. These results demonstrate that security boundaries must be enforced in application code, not by the model being attacked. Until such defenses are verified by tools like Swept AI, AI systems handling sensitive operations should be restricted to internal, trusted personnel.

</details>


### [181] [System-aware contextual digital twin for ICS anomaly diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24051)
*Eungyu Woo,Yooshin Kim,Wonje Heo,Donghoon Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合轻量级在线检测与LLM增强数字孪生的无监督ICS异常诊断框架，实现实时检测与可解释性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICS异常检测方法存在局限性：监督方法依赖大量标注数据且面临类别不平衡，基于模型的方法缺乏对异常根因的深度洞察，导致误报率高、操作员难以快速响应。

Method: 提出系统感知的无监督框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级在线检测模块，无需系统拓扑先验知识即可识别与正常行为的偏差；(2) 上下文数字孪生增强模块，结合大语言模型将检测证据转化为可解释的诊断假设与验证步骤。

Result: 在公开ICS基准测试上实现实时检测效率，提供一致且可解释的异常诊断结果，支持低延迟告警。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在复杂工业环境中实现低延迟告警与实用化部署，有效解决了现有方法在可解释性和实时性上的不足。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS) integrate computing, physical processes, and communication to operate critical infrastructures such as power grids, water treatment plants, and oil and gas facilities. As ICS become increasingly targeted by cyberattacks, timely and reliable anomaly diagnosis is essential for protecting operational safety. However, existing ICS anomaly detection approaches face practical limitations: supervised methods require extensive labeled attack data and suffer from class imbalance, while model-based detectors often lack the ability to provide deep insight into the root causes of anomalies, leading to elevated false alarms and making it difficult for operators to initiate a timely response. In this work, we propose a system-aware unsupervised framework for ICS anomaly diagnosis that combines lightweight online detection with contextual explanation. The system identifies deviations from observed normal behaviors without prior knowledge of system topology. To support actionable response, we further concatenate a contextual digital twin augmented with an Large Language Model (LLM) to enhance interpretability, which translates detection evidence into grounded diagnostic hypotheses and verification steps for operators. Experiments on public ICS benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves real-time detection efficiency and provides consistent, interpretable anomaly diagnoses, enabling low-latency warning and practical deployment in complex industrial environments.

</details>


### [182] [Listen to the Voices of Everyday Users: Democratizing Privacy Ratings for Sensitive Data Access in Mobile Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24066)
*Liu Wang,Tianshu Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种民主化隐私评估的新范式，通过原型系统DePRa让普通用户参与评估移动应用的数据访问必要性，实验表明该方法能有效补充专家审计，具有可行性和推广潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用频繁请求过度数据访问引发隐私担忧，现有GDPR等法规强调数据最小化但缺乏具体执行指导，专家驱动的审计机制在可扩展性、中立性和与用户期望一致性方面存在局限。

Method: 提出民主化隐私评估范式，开发DePRa原型系统，采用参与式设计，包含上下文解释提供、基于类别的代表性选择、直观评分界面和偏好驱动的评分调整四个功能模块，并在200名真实用户中进行评估。

Result: 实验表明DePRa能有效捕获用户对敏感数据访问的意见，用户评分与专家评估具有可比性，基于风险偏好的评分校准可行，验证了民主化隐私评估的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 民主化隐私评估范式能够有效补充现有专家审计机制，推动更具包容性的隐私评估实践，为数据最小化原则的具体落地提供了新的可操作路径。

Abstract: Mobile apps frequently request excessive data access, raising significant privacy concerns. While regulations like GDPR emphasize data minimization, they provide limited guidance on concretely defining and enforcing necessary data access. Existing regulatory mechanisms primarily rely on expert-driven audits that face challenges in scalability, neutrality, and alignment with user expectations. In this paper, we propose a novel paradigm--democratizing privacy assessment, inspired by prior work on user-centric privacy perceptions--which repositions users as active evaluators in the privacy auditing process, recognizing that user perceptions of data usage play a crucial role in assessing the appropriateness and necessity of data access. To operationalize this paradigm, we introduce DePRa, a prototype system developed through participatory design, featuring contextual explanation provision, category-based representative selection, an intuitive rating interface, and preference-based rating adjustment. We evaluated DePRa with 200 everyday mobile app users, analyzing how effectively it captures user opinions on sensitive data access, comparing their privacy ratings with expert assessments, and exploring risk preference-based score calibration. Our findings show the feasibility and promise of democratized privacy assessment, highlighting its potential to complement expert auditing and support inclusive privacy evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [Jailbreaking Frontier Foundation Models Through Intention Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24082)
*Xinhe Wang,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的多轮越狱攻击方法，利用用户意图反转欺骗模型。该方法通过逐步建立对话信任，利用模型的一致性属性，最终引导目标模型生成有害输出。此外，本文还发现了一种此前未被注意的漏洞——"平行越狱"（para-jailbreaking），即模型虽未直接回复有害内容，但所披露的信息仍然有害。该方法在GPT-5-thinking、Claude-Sonnet-4.5等前沿模型上取得了高成功率，并在多模态VLM模型上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全训练方法基于用户意图进行二元拒绝边界学习，但存在两个核心问题：(1) 二元训练范式导致脆弱性，攻击者可通过混淆意图绕过；(2) 使系统显得不够有用。前沿模型如GPT-5已转向"安全补全"机制（在遵守安全约束下最大化有用性），但这引入了新的漏洞——当用户伪装其意图为良性时（即意图反转），模型可能被利用。特别在多轮对话场景中，攻击者可通过多次交互强化其欺骗性良性意图，逐步突破安全防线。

Method: 提出一种多轮越狱攻击方法：(1) 通过模拟看似良性的意图，逐步建立对话信任；(2) 利用模型的"一致性属性"（模型倾向于保持前后对话一致），引导模型逐渐输出有害内容。核心创新在于利用多轮对话中累积的上下文信任，而非单轮直接攻击。此外，该方法还发现了"平行越狱"这一新型漏洞类别。

Result: (1) 在GPT-5-thinking和Claude-Sonnet-4.5等前沿模型上取得了高成功率；(2) 揭示并处理了平行越狱有害输出；(3) 在多模态VLM模型上的实验表现优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 当前基于"安全补全"的防御范式在多轮对话场景下存在严重的安全漏洞，意图反转攻击有效。同时，平行越狱的存在表明模型的安全评估需要从"直接有害输出"扩展到"间接有害信息泄露"。研究呼吁更鲁棒的多轮对话安全机制。
