Abstract: Large (vision-)language models exhibit remarkable capability but remain highly susceptible to jailbreaking. Existing safety training approaches aim to have the model learn a refusal boundary between safe and unsafe, based on the user's intent. It has been found that this binary training regime often leads to brittleness, since the user intent cannot reliably be evaluated, especially if the attacker obfuscates their intent, and also makes the system seem unhelpful. In response, frontier models, such as GPT-5, have shifted from refusal-based safeguards to safe completion, that aims to maximize helpfulness while obeying safety constraints. However, safe completion could be exploited when a user pretends their intention is benign. Specifically, this intent inversion would be effective in multi-turn conversation, where the attacker has multiple opportunities to reinforce their deceptively benign intent. In this work, we introduce a novel multi-turn jailbreaking method that exploits this vulnerability. Our approach gradually builds conversational trust by simulating benign-seeming intentions and by exploiting the consistency property of the model, ultimately guiding the target model toward harmful, detailed outputs. Most crucially, our approach also uncovered an additional class of model vulnerability that we call para-jailbreaking that has been unnoticed up to now. Para-jailbreaking describes the situation where the model may not reveal harmful direct reply to the attack query, however the information that it reveals is nevertheless harmful. Our contributions are threefold. First, it achieves high success rates against frontier models including GPT-5-thinking and Claude-Sonnet-4.5. Second, our approach revealed and addressed para-jailbreaking harmful output. Third, experiments on multimodal VLM models showed that our approach outperformed state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [184] [Evaluating Cryptographic API Misuse Detectors for Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24085)
*Vivi Andersson,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Go语言中密码学API误用检测进行了首次全面研究，评估了4种主流工具（CodeQL、Gopher、Gosec和Snyk Code），建立了包含14类误用的统一分类体系，并在328个安全关键型开源Go项目中发现了7,473个密码学API误用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学API误用是破坏软件安全基础的关键漏洞类别，但尽管Go语言在安全关键型基础设施中占据主导地位，该领域在Go中仍缺乏系统性的探索和研究。

Method: 首先识别并分析了4种最先进的检测工具（CodeQL、Gopher、Gosec和Snyk Code），进而建立包含14类相关误用的统一分类体系，最后对328个安全关键型开源Go项目进行实验评估。

Result: 在328个开源Go项目中发现了7,473个密码学API误用实例，揭示了这些漏洞的普遍性和分布特征。系统比较显示不同工具在误用覆盖范围上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究为安全工程师提供了即时的实践指导意义，并为该领域的长期研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Cryptographic API misuse represents a critical vulnerability class that undermines the security foundations of modern software. Yet, it remains largely unexplored in Go despite its dominance in security-critical infrastructure. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of cryptographic API misuse detection in Go, identifying and analyzing 4 state-of-the-art tools (CodeQL, Gopher, Gosec, and Snyk Code) and establishing a consolidated taxonomy of 14 relevant misuse classes. Through an experimental evaluation of 328 security-critical open-source Go projects, we discovered 7,473 cryptographic API misuses, providing insights into the prevalence and distribution of these vulnerabilities. Our systematic comparison reveals significant variations in misuse coverage, with immediate practical implications for security engineers and long-term implications for research in this domain.

</details>


### [185] [Detecting Avalanche Effect in Adversarial Settings: Spotting the Encryption Loops in Ransomware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24131)
*Nanqing Luo,Xusheng Li,Haizhou Wang,Shuangyi Zhu,Yuan Ma,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于雪崩效应检测的勒索软件加密循环识别方法，通过Shapiro-Wilk正态性检验实现记录-重放检测机制，在对抗性环境下达到0.0%假阴性率和1.1%假阳性率，成功分析了10个代表性勒索软件家族的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（如CipherXRay）仅检测雪崩效应的必要条件"涟漪效应"，而非雪崩效应本身，存在被对抗性攻击绕过的风险。亟需一种直接检测雪崩效应本身、且能容忍不准确I/O识别和对抗性规避的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出记录-重放检测机制，利用Shapiro-Wilk正态性检验的统计保证，通过检查密文比特变化是否符合正态分布来判断是否存在真正的雪崩效应，从而识别加密循环。

Result: 实验实现0.0%假阴性率和1.1%假阳性率；对10个真实勒索软件家族样本的反向工程分析全部成功。

Conclusion: 直接检测雪崩效应本身比检测其必要条件（涟漪效应）更可靠，统计检验方法能有效抵御对抗性攻击，为二进制勒索软件分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Spotting encryption loops in binary-only ransomware is a critical reverse engineering task. Since the existence of avalanche effect, an intrinsic characteristic of any secure encryption algorithms, is unavoidable during a victim data encryption attack, it is a very promising direction to spot encryption loops through avalanche effect detection. Unfortunately, no existing work in this direction ensures that the being-checked effect is the avalanche effect itself. Although CipherXRay is inspired by avalanche effect, it only checks whether a "ripple effect" (i.e., a necessary but non-sufficient condition) of avalanche effect exists, allowing a straightforward counterattack to succeed. In this work, we present a new approach that checks the avalanche effect itself. Because the detection is conducted in adversarial settings (e.g., the ransomware author may obfuscate the code), a viable approach must tolerate inaccurate input \& output identification and must be resilient to adversarial evasion. These challenges are addressed by a novel record-and-replay detection mechanism that takes advantage of the statistical guarantees provided by the Shapiro-Wilk normality test. The experimental results show that our approach achieves 0.0\% false negative rate and 1.1\% false positive rate. When our tool is employed to reverse engineer real-world ransomware samples, it succeeds in analyzing all the ransomware samples selected from ten representative families.

</details>


### [186] [Defusing the Trigger: Plug-and-Play Defense for Backdoored LLMs via Tail-Risk Intrinsic Geometric Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24162)
*Kaisheng Fan,Weizhe Zhang,Yishu Gao,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Xunzhu Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Tail-risk Intrinsic Geometric Smoothing (TIGS)，一种即插即用的推理时防御方法，通过内在几何平滑技术在不更新参数、无需外部数据的情况下，有效抑制大语言模型的后门攻击，同时保持模型原有推理能力和语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型后门攻击的防御方法存在两难困境：离线净化方法准备成本高且损害模型效用，在线干预方法引入严重延迟。需要一种兼顾安全性、效用性和低延迟的实用防御方案。

Method: TIGS完全在原生前向传播中运行，首先通过内容感知的尾风险筛查识别可疑注意力头和行，然后应用内在几何平滑：弱内容域校正保留语义锚点，强全行收缩破坏触发器主导的路由，最后通过受控的全行写回重构注意力矩阵以保证推理稳定性。

Result: 大量实验表明TIGS能显著抑制攻击成功率，同时严格保持干净推理能力和开放端语义一致性。该安全-效用-延迟均衡在包括密集模型、推理导向模型和稀疏混合专家模型在内的多种架构上均有效。

Conclusion: 通过以极小的延迟开销结构性破坏对抗路由，TIGS为最先进的大语言模型建立了一种高度实用、可部署的防御标准。

Abstract: Defending against backdoor attacks in large language models remains a critical practical challenge. Existing defenses mitigate these threats but typically incur high preparation costs and degrade utility via offline purification, or introduce severe latency via complex online interventions. To overcome this dichotomy, we present Tail-risk Intrinsic Geometric Smoothing (TIGS), a plug-and-play inference-time defense requiring no parameter updates, external clean data, or auxiliary generation. TIGS leverages the observation that successful backdoor triggers consistently induce localized attention collapse within the semantic content region. Operating entirely within the native forward pass, TIGS first performs content-aware tail-risk screening to identify suspicious attention heads and rows using sample-internal signals. It then applies intrinsic geometric smoothing: a weak content-domain correction preserves semantic anchoring, while a stronger full-row contraction disrupts trigger-dominant routing. Finally, a controlled full-row write-back reconstructs the attention matrix to ensure inference stability. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TIGS substantially suppresses attack success rates while strictly preserving clean reasoning and open-ended semantic consistency. Crucially, this favorable security-utility-latency equilibrium persists across diverse architectures, including dense, reasoning-oriented, and sparse mixture-of-experts models. By structurally disrupting adversarial routing with marginal latency overhead, TIGS establishes a highly practical, deployment-ready defense standard for state-of-the-art LLMs.

</details>


### [187] [Agentic Witnessing: Pragmatic and Scalable TEE-Enabled Privacy-Preserving Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24203)
*Antony Rowstron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"Agentic Witnessing"框架，利用TEE中的LLM审计器，通过布尔查询对私有数据集进行定性验证，并在无需数据披露的前提下生成加密推理轨迹。在21篇计算机科学论文的代码库上验证了该架构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计专有数据的语义属性存在根本性矛盾：验证需要透明访问，而专有权要求保密性。零知识证明虽能保证隐私，但仅限于精确代数约束，无法验证定性的非结构化属性（如代码库中的逻辑）。

Method: 提出Agentic Witnessing框架，由Verifier、Prover和Auditor三个智能体组成。Verifier可向Auditor提问有限数量的二元真/假问题。Auditor基于LLM并隔离在可信执行环境(TEE)中，利用Model Context Protocol (MCP)动态检查目标数据集，生成yes/no裁决及加密转录（包含推理轨迹的签名哈希链，绑定原始数据集和TEE硬件信任根）。

Result: 对21篇同行评审计算机科学论文的公开GitHub代码库自动执行artifact评估，验证了出版物中描述的五个高层属性，将源代码视为私有数据。结果表明TEE支持的智能体审计实现了隐私保护的监督机制。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解耦了定性验证与数据披露需求，为隐私保护下的专有数据语义属性审计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Auditing the semantic properties of proprietary data creates a fundamental tension: verification requires transparent access, while proprietary rights demand confidentiality. While Zero-Knowledge Proofs (ZKPs) ensure privacy, they are typically limited to precise algebraic constraints and are ill-suited for verifying qualitative, unstructured properties, such as the logic within a codebase. We propose {\em Agentic Witnessing}, a framework that moves verification from attested execution to {\em attested reasoning}. The system is composed of three agents: a Verifier (who wants to check properties of a dataset), a Prover (who owns the dataset) and an Auditor (that inspects the dataset). The Verifier is allowed to ask a limited number of simple binary true/false questions to the auditor. By isolating an LLM-based Auditor within a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE), the system enables the Verifier to query a Prover's private data via simple Boolean queries, without exposing the raw dataset. The Auditor uses the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to dynamically inspect the target dataset, producing a yes/no verdict accompanied by a cryptographic transcript: a signed hash chain binding the reasoning trace to both the original dataset and the TEE's hardware root of trust. We demonstrate this architecture by automating the artifact evaluation process for 21 peer-reviewed computer science papers with released codebases on GitHub (e.g. Does the codebase implement the system described in the paper?). We verified five high-level properties of these codebases described in the corresponding publications, treating the source code as private. Our results show that TEE-enabled agentic auditing provides a mechanism for privacy-preserving oversight, effectively decoupling qualitative verification from the need for data disclosure.

</details>


### [188] [Converging Zero Trust and IoT Security: A Multivocal Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24205)
*Mariam Wehbe,Laurent Bobelin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过多视角文献综述（68篇学术+36篇工业研究），系统分析了物联网安全与零信任原则的融合现状，发现学术界侧重通过修改物联网设备以符合零信任原则，而工业界更关注在现有零信任框架内进行实用集成，同时揭示了社会技术理解、成本效益评估和跨学科协作等关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网安全与零信任原则的融合是一个新兴趋势，但缺乏全面、多视角的系统性分析，现有研究视角单一，未能充分整合学术界与工业界的差异化观点。

Method: 采用多视角文献综述方法，综合分析了68篇学术研究和36篇工业研究报告，从学术和工业两个互补但不同的视角进行对比研究。

Result: 学术界聚焦于通过修改物联网系统使其符合零信任原则；工业界则优先考虑在现有零信任框架内进行实用集成，并遵循NIST标准。两者存在显著视角差异。

Conclusion: 社会技术理解、成本效益评估和跨学科协作是当前研究的主要空白，也是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: The convergence of Internet of Things (IoT) security and Zero Trust (ZT) principles is a trending topic, demanding a comprehensive, multi-perspective analysis. We present the first multivocal literature review (MLR) on this topic, combining 68 academic and 36 industrial studies. This comprehensive review identifies two complementary yet divergent perspectives: academia focuses on IoT compliance with ZT principles through IoT modifications, while industry prioritizes practical integration within existing ZT frameworks guided by NIST standards. The analysis reveals critical research gaps in socio-technical understanding, cost-benefit evaluation, and interdisciplinary collaboration, highlighting these as key directions for future research.

</details>


### [189] [Resolving Conflicts Between RTOS Timekeeping and Uninterruptable Trusted Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24277)
*Antonio Joia Neto,Amarin Amarin,Norrathep Rattanavipanon,Ivan De Oliveira Nunes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM TrustZone-M微控制器上Secure World与Non-Secure RTOS之间因SysTick中断处理冲突导致的实时性问题，提出了一种安全驱动的时间同步机制，通过Secure World测量经过时间并透明补偿Non-Secure RTOS丢失的时钟滴答数，在不修改RTOS、不引入显著开销的前提下实现了跨世界的一致单调时间同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低功耗微控制器的TEE（如ARM TrustZone-M）中，Secure World需要原子性执行安全服务，无法处理Non-Secure RTOS依赖的周期性SysTick中断，导致RTOS时间感知中断、实时行为受损。现有方案缺乏对这类中断冲突的系统性分析和解决方案。

Method: 提出一种Secure-driven时间同步机制：Secure World在进入安全服务时记录时间，在执行完成后计算经过时间，在恢复Non-Secure执行前将丢失的SysTick数量直接写入RTOS的时间维护数据结构，实现无侵入的时间补偿。

Result: 该方法恢复了跨世界的一致单调时间，无需修改底层RTOS代码，且无显著运行时开销。

Conclusion: 该机制有效解决了TEE微控制器上安全服务与实时RTOS之间因SysTick中断共享冲突导致的时间一致性问题，实现了安全与实时的共存。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) on low-power microcontrollers (e.g., ARM TrustZone-M) enable isolation of Secure and Non-Secure software but still require both worlds to share resources, including interrupt controllers. In this model, real-time applications and real-time operating systems (RTOS-s) are executed in the Non-Secure sub-system, whereas the Secure sub-system is typically reserved for a small set of pre-defined security (e.g., cryptographic) operations referred to as trusted computing services. However, many RTOS-s rely on periodic interrupts (SysTicks) to advance their own notion of time (time-keeping), and the delivery of this interrupt is essential for preserving real-time behavior. On the other hand, the security of many trusted computing services requires atomicity vis-a-vis the Non-Secure sub-system (where the RTOS resides), precluding SysTick handling.
  This paper first characterizes this conflict and then introduces a Secure-driven time synchronization mechanism in which the Secure World measures elapsed time and compensates the Non-Secure RTOS by unobtrusively updating the RTOS time-keeping data structures with the appropriate number of missed ticks before re-enabling interrupts and resuming the execution of the Non-Secure system. This approach restores a consistent, monotonic notion of time across worlds and enables secure coexistence of trusted computing services and RTOS-s on microcontrollers. Importantly, the proposed approach requires no modifications to the underlying RTOS and yields no significant run-time overhead.

</details>


### [190] [RowHammer Vulnerability Counter (RVC): Redefining RowHammer Detection with Victim-Centric Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24287)
*Lavi Jain,Venkata Kalyan Tavva*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RVC框架，通过评估行实际比特翻转脆弱性而非激活次数来缓解Rowhammer漏洞，相比Graphene减少95-99.99%的不必要刷新，提升能效并降低76.91% LLC延迟，且无额外空间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rowhammer缓解技术（如Graphene、Twice、Hydra）依赖跟踪每行激活次数并在达到阈值时发起刷新，但存在识别真正有比特翻转风险的行效率低下的问题，且此前工作错误设置了跟踪阈值导致安全缺陷。

Method: 提出Rowhammer脆弱性计数（RVC）框架，从追踪激活次数转向评估行的实际比特翻转脆弱性，仅对即将发生比特翻转的行选择性发起刷新，从而大幅减少不必要的刷新操作。

Result: 相比Graphene，RVC在缓解诱导刷新方面提升95-99.99%，无额外空间开销；能效提升，平均LLC延迟降低高达76.91%。

Conclusion: RVC在准确性和效率上均优于现有方法，是一种高效、可扩展的现代DRAM系统Rowhammer防护方案。

Abstract: The Rowhammer vulnerability poses an increasing challenge with newer generations of DRAM and aggressive technology scaling. Existing mitigation techniques, such as Graphene, Twice, and Hydra, primarily rely on tracking activation counts for each row and issuing refreshes when a row reaches a predefined tracking threshold. However, these methods have inherent limitations, including inefficiencies in identifying rows genuinely at risk of bit flips.
  In this paper, we propose a novel framework called Rowhammer Vulnerability Count (RVC), which shifts the focus from activation count tracking to evaluating a row's actual vulnerability to bit flips. By selectively issuing refreshes only to rows on the verge of experiencing bit flips, RVC drastically reduces unnecessary refresh operations. We also demonstrate that prior works have incorrectly set tracking thresholds, leading to security flaws.
  Our evaluation shows that RVC achieves 95 - 99.99% improvement in mitigation induced refreshes when compared to Graphene, with no additional space overhead. Furthermore, RVC improves energy efficiency and reduces average LLC latency by up to 76.91%, making it a highly efficient and scalable solution for addressing Rowhammer in modern DRAM systems. These findings establish RVC as a superior approach for preventing Rowhammer, outperforming existing methods in both accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [191] [X-NegoBox: An Explainable Privacy-Budget Negotiation Framework for Secure Peer-to-Peer Energy Data Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24326)
*Poushali Sengupta,Sabita Maharjan,Frank Eliassen,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出X-NegoBox框架，通过可解释的隐私预算协商协议(APBNP)和解释层(X-Contract)，实现自适应的、可解释的能源数据共享决策，保护用户隐私的同时提高请求接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据共享机制依赖固定策略或预定义差分隐私预算，无法适应数据敏感性、可靠性和请求目的的变化，且缺乏解释能力，降低了用户信任和参与度。

Method: 提出X-NegoBox框架，包含：(1) 本地DataBox管理原始数据；(2) 自主隐私预算协商协议(APBNP)，基于信任度、特征敏感性、声明目的、历史行为和风险定价动态确定隐私预算；(3) 可解释协议层(X-Contract)生成人机可读的决策理由；(4) 沙箱执行环境确保只有脱敏输出被共享。

Result: 在真实能源市场场景下，X-NegoBox减少了隐私泄露，提高了请求接受率和可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过引入可解释的自适应隐私预算协商机制，X-NegoBox有效平衡了数据效用和隐私保护，增强了用户信任和参与度。

Abstract: The decentralization of modern energy systems is transforming consumers into prosumers who continuously exchange data with aggregators, peers, and market operators. While such data is essential for peer-to-peer trading, demand response, and distributed forecasting, it can reveal sensitive household patterns and introduce privacy risks. Existing data sharing mechanisms rely on fixed policies or predefined differential privacy budgets, limiting their ability to adapt to variations in reliability, data sensitivity, and request purpose. As a result, prosumers rarely receive explanations for why a request is accepted, rejected, or modified, reducing trust and participation.
  To address these limitations, we propose X-NegoBox, an explainable negotiation framework for adaptive privacy budgeting and transparent decision making. Each prosumer data is managed locally within a private DataBox, where raw data remain confined. Incoming requests are processed by an Autonomous Privacy Budget Negotiation Protocol (APBNP), which determines an appropriate privacy budget based on trust, feature sensitivity, declared purpose, historical behavior, and risk-aware pricing. When needed, APBNP generates privacy-preserving counter-offers, such as reduced resolution or duration.
  An Explainable Agreement Layer (X-Contract) produces human- and machine-readable justifications for each decision. After agreement, requester code executes locally in a sandbox, and only sanitized outputs are shared. Experiments on realistic energy market settings show reduced privacy leakage, higher acceptance rates, and improved interpretability.

</details>


### [192] [GoAT-X: A Graph of Auditing Thoughts for Securing Token Transactions in Cross-Chain Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24341)
*Zijun Feng,Yuming Feng,Yu Wang,Weizhe Zhang,Yuhong Nan,Yuang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GoAT-X是一个将跨链智能合约审计从启发式模式匹配转向系统性第一性原理验证的框架，通过图式审计思维约束LLM推理，在已知跨链代币交易攻击基准上达到92%的细粒度审计点召回率和95%的漏洞项目覆盖率，并在真实环境中发现117个确认风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥作为多链生态关键基础设施，因实现缺陷已造成超过28亿美元损失。现有基于字节码的静态分析无法处理跨链交互的语义复杂性，而基于LLM的方法虽能理解源代码，但在复杂多合约依赖上存在幻觉推理问题。

Method: GoAT-X将审计过程构建为审计思维图（Graph of Auditing Thoughts），模拟人类专家分解、推理和验证安全逻辑的方式。通过将LLM推理锚定在静态提取的数据流上，将抽象安全属性与具体代码实现显式关联，将语义推理约束在结构化和状态边界内。将缺失约束和对抗性绕过路径作为第一类漏洞目标，动态探索推理路径以识别可利用的语义间隙。

Result: 在涵盖所有已知跨链代币交易攻击的综合基准上，GoAT-X达到92%的细粒度审计点召回率和95%的漏洞项目覆盖率；在真实环境中识别出117个已确认风险，且运营成本较低。

Conclusion: GoAT-X为可扩展、逻辑驱动的跨链安全建立了新标准，证明将结构化数据流约束与LLM语义理解相结合可以有效解决跨链合约审计中的虚假推理问题。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges, the critical infrastructure of the multi-chain ecosystem, have become a primary target for attackers, resulting in over $2.8 billion in losses due to subtle implementation flaws. Existing defenses, such as bytecode-level static analysis, are ill-equipped to handle the semantic complexity of cross-chain interactions, while LLM-based approaches, which can understand source code, struggle with hallucinatory reasoning over complex, multi-contract dependencies. In this paper, we propose GoAT-X, a framework that shifts automated cross-chain smart contract codebases auditing from heuristic pattern matching toward systematic first-principles verification. GoAT-X structures the audit process as a Graph of Auditing Thoughts, explicitly mirroring how human experts decompose, reason about, and validate security logic. By anchoring LLM reasoning in statically extracted data flows and explicitly linking abstract security properties to concrete code implementations, the framework constrains semantic reasoning within well-defined structural and state boundaries. Within this constrained space, GoAT-X treats missing constraints and adversarial bypass paths in cross-chain logic as first-class vulnerability targets and dynamically explores reasoning paths to identify exploitable semantic gaps. We evaluate GoAT-X on a comprehensive benchmark covering all known cross-chain token transaction attacks. GoAT-X achieves 92% recall on fine-grained audit points and 95% coverage of vulnerable projects, while identifying 117 confirmed risks in the wild with low operational cost, establishing a new standard for scalable, logic-driven cross-chain security.

</details>


### [193] [Information-Theoretic Distributed Point Functions with Shorter Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24385)
*Hang Deng,Liang Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于Ghasemi等人的PIR方案，提出了一种新的份额转换方法，构建了在输出群G=Z_p上完全安全的1-私有ITDPF，其密钥渐近长度更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有完全安全的ITDPF方案在相同输出群下存在密钥长度不够高效的局限性，本文旨在通过新的份额转换方法提升效率。

Method: 基于Ghasemi、Kopparty和Sudan（STOC 2025）的私有信息检索（PIR）方案，设计了一种新颖的份额转换方法，构造了完全安全的1-私有信息论分布式点函数。

Result: 提出了一个针对输出群G=Z_p（p为任意素数）的完全安全1-私有ITDPF，其密钥长度渐近地优于现有同类方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ITDPF在保证完全安全的前提下，实现了更短的密钥长度，提升了效率。

Abstract: A t-private n-server Information-Theoretic Distributed Point Function ((t,n)-ITDPF) allows one to convert any point function f_{alpha,beta}(x): [N] -> G into n shares (secret keys), such that each server can compute an additive share of f_{alpha,beta}(x) with a key while any <= t servers learn absolutely no information about the function. This paper constructs a novel share conversion based on the private information retrieval (PIR) of Ghasemi, Kopparty, and Sudan (STOC 2025) and proposes a perfectly secure 1-private ITDPF with output group G = Z_p, where p can be any prime. Compared with the existing perfectly secure ITDPFs for the same output group, the proposed ITDPF is more efficient with asymptotically shorter secret keys.

</details>


### [194] [MAS-SZZ: Multi-Agentic SZZ Algorithm for Vulnerability-Inducing Commit Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24398)
*Sicong Cao,Jinxuan Xu,Le Yu,Jing Yang,Xingwei Lin,Linlin Zhu,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体SZZ算法MAS-SZZ，通过智能体协作识别漏洞引入提交，在多个数据集和编程语言上性能大幅超越现有方法，F1分数最高提升65.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SZZ算法（包括V-SZZ和LLM4SZZ）由于锚点选择错误和回溯能力不足，在漏洞引入提交识别任务上表现不佳，无法满足实际使用的可靠性需求。

Method: MAS-SZZ通过多智能体协作实现：1) 利用CVE描述和修复提交总结漏洞根因；2) 采用结构化逐步提示策略，基于每个补丁块的变化意图定位漏洞相关语句；3) 以这些脆弱语句为锚点，自主回溯仓库历史找到首次引入漏洞的提交。

Result: 在多个数据集和编程语言上，MAS-SZZ全面超越现有基线方法，相较于最佳SZZ算法，F1分数最高提升65.22%。

Conclusion: MAS-SZZ通过多智能体协作有效解决了锚点选择和回溯能力的瓶颈，为漏洞引入提交识别提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability-inducing commit identification serves as a foundation for a series of software security tasks, such as vulnerability detection and affected version analysis. A straightforward solution is the SZZ algorithm, which traces back through the code history to identify the earliest commit that modify the vulnerable code. Unfortunately, neither the customized V-SZZ nor state-of-the-art LLM4SZZ perform satisfactorily due to the incorrect anchor selection and inadequate backtracking capability, making them far beyond a reliable usage in practice.
  To overcome these challenges, we propose a multi-agentic SZZ algorithm, named MAS-SZZ, that facilitates the identification of vulnerability-inducing commits through collaboration among agents. Specifically, given a CVE description and its corresponding fixing commit, MAS-SZZ summarizes the root cause of the vulnerability and employs a structured step-forward prompting strategy to localize vulnerability-related statements based on the change intent of each patch hunk. These vulnerable statements serve as anchors from which MAS-SZZ autonomously traces backward through the repository's history to find the commit that first introduced the vulnerability. Extensive experiments show that MAS-SZZ outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines across datasets and programming languages, achieving F1-score gains of up to 65.22% over the best-performing SZZ algorithm.

</details>


### [195] [A Survey on Split Learning for LLM Fine-Tuning: Models, Systems, and Privacy Optimizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24468)
*Zihan Liu,Yizhen Wang,Rui Wang,Xiu Tang,Sai Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了针对大语言模型微调的拆分学习技术，从模型优化、系统效率和隐私保护三个维度系统梳理了现有工作，并提出了统一的细粒度训练流水线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调计算成本高，资源受限组织难以承担；云端虽能提供算力，但数据隐私问题阻碍了敏感信息的第三方共享。拆分学习作为一种协作且安全的训练范式，有望解决这一矛盾，但该领域缺乏系统性的综述研究。

Method: 提出统一的细粒度训练流水线来定位关键操作组件，从模型级优化、系统级效率、隐私保护三个核心维度对现有工作进行系统综述，并构建结构化分类体系。

Result: 完成了首个针对大语言模型微调拆分学习的全面综述，建立了涵盖多种模型方法、系统优化和隐私攻防技术的分类体系。

Conclusion: 该综述为推进可扩展、鲁棒且安全的协作式大语言模型适配奠定了基础，为后续研究提供了清晰的分类比较和方向指引。

Abstract: Fine-tuning unlocks large language models (LLMs) for specialized applications, but its high computational cost often puts it out of reach for resource-constrained organizations. While cloud platforms could provide the needed resources, data privacy concerns make sharing sensitive information with third parties risky. A promising solution is split learning for LLM fine-tuning, which divides the model between clients and a server, allowing collaborative and secure training through exchanged intermediate data, thus enabling resource-constrained participants to adapt LLMs safely. % In light of this, a growing body of literature has emerged to advance this paradigm, introducing varied model methods, system optimizations, and privacy defense-attack techniques for split learning. To bring clarity and direction to the field, a comprehensive survey is needed to classify, compare, and critique these diverse approaches. This paper fills the gap by presenting the first extensive survey dedicated to split learning for LLM fine-tuning. We propose a unified, fine-grained training pipeline to pinpoint key operational components and conduct a systematic review of state-of-the-art work across three core dimensions: model-level optimization, system-level efficiency, and privacy preservation. Through this structured taxonomy, we establish a foundation for advancing scalable, robust, and secure collaborative LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [196] [Layerwise Convergence Fingerprints for Runtime Misbehavior Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24542)
*Nay Myat Min,Long H. Pham,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需调优的运行时监控方法LCF，通过计算层间隐藏状态轨迹的Mahalanobis距离来检测后门攻击、越狱攻击和提示注入三类威胁，在多种模型上实现了高检测率和极低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时防御方法针对后门、越狱、提示注入等威胁逐一设计，且通常依赖干净的参考模型、触发器知识或可编辑权重，这些假设对于不透明的第三方大模型制品难以满足。

Method: LCF将层间隐藏状态轨迹作为健康信号，计算每层间差异的对角Mahalanobis距离，通过Ledoit-Wolf收缩进行聚合，并利用留一法在200个干净样本上校准阈值。该方法无需参考模型、触发器知识或重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B、Qwen2.5-14B四个架构上，后门攻击成功率降至1%以下（Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma-2）和1.3%（Qwen2.5-14B）；DAN越狱检测率92-100%，GCG和角色扮演越狱62-100%；文本载荷注入检测率100%；后门假阳性率12-16%，推理开销小于0.1%。

Conclusion: 单个聚合评分即可覆盖三类威胁家族，无需针对特定威胁调优，LCF可作为面向云端和端侧LLM的通用运行时安全层。

Abstract: Large language models deployed at runtime can misbehave in ways that clean-data validation cannot anticipate: training-time backdoors lie dormant until triggered, jailbreaks subvert safety alignment, and prompt injections override the deployer's instructions. Existing runtime defenses address these threats one at a time and often assume a clean reference model, trigger knowledge, or editable weights, assumptions that rarely hold for opaque third-party artifacts. We introduce Layerwise Convergence Fingerprinting (LCF), a tuning-free runtime monitor that treats the inter-layer hidden-state trajectory as a health signal: LCF computes a diagonal Mahalanobis distance on every inter-layer difference, aggregates via Ledoit-Wolf shrinkage, and thresholds via leave-one-out calibration on 200 clean examples, with no reference model, trigger knowledge, or retraining. Evaluated on four architectures (Llama-3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B, Qwen2.5-14B) across backdoors, jailbreaks, and prompt injection (56 backdoor combinations, 3 jailbreak techniques, and BIPIA email + code-QA), LCF reduces mean backdoor attack success rate (ASR) below 1% on Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma-2 and to 1.3% on Qwen2.5-14B, detects 92-100% of DAN jailbreaks (62-100% for GCG and softer role-play), and flags 100% of text-payload injections across all eight (model, domain) cells, at 12-16% backdoor FPR and <0.1% inference overhead. A single aggregation score covers all three threat families without threat-specific tuning, positioning LCF as a general-purpose runtime safety layer for cloud-served and on-device LLMs.

</details>


### [197] [DETOUR: A Practical Backdoor Attack against Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24599)
*Dazhuang Liu,Yanqi Qiao,Rui Wang,Kaitai Liang,Georgios Smaragdakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DETOUR，一种利用语义触发器（如真实物体在不同视角下的图案）对DETR目标检测模型进行后门攻击的方法。针对现有补丁式触发器忽略真实世界中尺寸、视场和位置变化的问题，发现补丁触发器在相邻位置存在"触发器辐射效应"（TRE），并通过多位置协同增强TRE，实现跨图像的高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DETR后门攻击依赖固定位置、极小扰动的补丁触发器，忽略了真实世界中触发器可能出现不同尺寸、视场和位置，且极小扰动难以被相机捕捉，限制了攻击实用性。

Method: 1) 发现补丁触发器在DETR中相邻位置激活后门时存在"触发器辐射效应"（TRE）；2) 在多位置插入补丁触发器协同增强TRE；3) 训练时将触发器缩放至不同尺寸并插入多个预定义位置；4) 从真实物体（如杯子）在多视场下提取图案进行触发器注入。

Result: 所提方法在不同视场和空间配置下均可可靠激活后门，实现实用性的物理世界攻击效果。

Conclusion: DETOUR通过语义触发器结合多尺寸、多位置注入及多视场提取策略，有效解决了真实场景中DETR后门攻击的实用性问题。

Abstract: Object detection (OD) is critical to real-world vision systems, yet existing backdoor attacks on detection transformers (DETRs) for OD tasks rely on patch-wise triggers optimized at fixed locations with minimal perturbations. Such attacks overlook that backdoor triggers in the real world may appear at different sizes, fields of view (FoVs), and locations in images, while minimal perturbations are difficult for cameras to capture, limiting attack practicality. We first observe that a patch-wise trigger in DETR delivers high attack effectiveness when activating the backdoor across neighboring locations, a phenomenon we term the trigger radiating effect (TRE). Meanwhile, inserting patch-wise triggers across multiple locations synergistically enhances TRE, resulting in high attack effectiveness across images. We propose DETOUR, a practical backdoor attack by using semantic triggers that are effective in real-world object detection systems. To ensure attack practicality, we rescale trigger patterns to different sizes and insert them at various predefined locations during backdoor training, enabling the model to recognize the trigger regardless of its spatial configurations. To address FoV variations in physical deployments, we extract the trigger pattern from a real-world object (e.g., a mug) captured under multiple FoVs and inject the trigger accordingly, promoting viewpoint-invariant backdoor activation and enhancing TRE across the entire image. As a result, the backdoor can be reliably activated under diverse FoVs and spatial configurations.

</details>


### [198] [AgentWard: A Lifecycle Security Architecture for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24657)
*Yixiang Zhang,Xinhao Deng,Jiaqing Wu,Yue Xiao,Ke Xu,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentWard提出了一种面向自主AI智能体生命周期的纵深防御架构，将安全防护系统性地组织在初始化、输入处理、记忆、决策和执行五个阶段，通过跨层协调的异构控制实现威胁传播拦截和关键资产保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体将大语言模型扩展为完整运行时系统，涉及技能加载、外部内容摄入、记忆维护、多步骤规划和工具调用等多个环节，安全威胁可在不同接口间传播并在环境中造成危害，亟需系统化的全生命周期防护架构。

Method: 提出AgentWard架构，采用生命周期导向的纵深防御设计，在初始化、输入处理、记忆、决策和执行五个阶段分别部署异构安全控制，并通过跨层协调机制实现威胁传播路径拦截。在OpenClaw上实现了插件式原型系统。

Result: 在OpenClaw上实现了可运行的插件式原型，验证了跨五阶段纵深防御架构的可行性，为自主AI智能体的运行时安全控制、信任传播管理和执行隔离提供了具体蓝图。

Conclusion: AgentWard为自主AI智能体提供了一种具体的运行时安全控制蓝图，能够系统性地组织多阶段防护、管理信任传播并强制执行隔离，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents extend large language models into full runtime systems that load skills, ingest external content, maintain memory, plan multi-step actions, and invoke privileged tools. In such systems, security failures rarely remain confined to a single interface; instead, they can propagate across initialization, input processing, memory, decision-making, and execution, often becoming apparent only when harmful effects materialize in the environment. This paper presents AgentWard, a lifecycle-oriented, defense-in-depth architecture that systematically organizes protection across these five stages. AgentWard integrates stage-specific, heterogeneous controls with cross-layer coordination, enabling threats to be intercepted along their propagation paths while safeguarding critical assets. We detail the design rationale and architecture of five coordinated protection layers, and implement a plugin-native prototype on OpenClaw to demonstrate practical feasibility. This perspective provides a concrete blueprint for structuring runtime security controls, managing trust propagation, and enforcing execution containment in autonomous AI agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/FIND-Lab/AgentWard .

</details>


### [199] [Machine-Checked Cardinality Bounds for Masked Barrett Reduction: A 1-Bit Side-Channel Leakage Barrier in Post-Quantum Cryptographic Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24670)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提供了首个经过机器验证的Barrett约简在素数域一阶算术掩码下的泄露分析，证明其内部线路的最大重数为2（即最多泄露1比特最小熵），并提出了PF-PINI组合框架，所有结论均在Lean 4中完成形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码组合框架（ISW、t-SNI、PINI、DOM）仅针对GF(2)上的布尔掩码，缺乏对基于NTT的后量子密码实现中Barrett约简在素数域一阶算术掩码和探针模型下的泄露刻画。NIST IR 8547建议对PQC实现采用形式化方法验证，而此前系列工作中Adams Bridge因缺乏掩码组合违反了PF-PINI，导致漏洞。

Method: 1) 证明Barrett内部线路映射$f_x(m) = ((x + 2^s - m) \bmod 2^s) \bmod q$的原像基数属于{0,1,2}，称为"1比特屏障"；2) 引入PF-PINI（素数域PINI），证明Barrett满足PF-PINI(2)，Cooley-Tukey蝶形满足PF-PINI(1)；3) 在Lean 4 + Mathlib中完成12个定理的形式化证明，零个sorry，对任意q>0普遍成立。

Result: 1) 证明了Barrett内部线路最大泄露不超过1比特最小熵（保守上界），且某些不可达输出值使实际泄露更小；2) 若引入新鲜级间掩码，组合管线的最大重数为$\max(k_1, k_2)$，1比特屏障可传播；3) 解释了Adams Bridge因缺乏新鲜级间掩码而违反PF-PINI组合导致漏洞。

Conclusion: 1比特屏障提供了首个针对ML-KEM (FIPS 203) 和 ML-DSA (FIPS 204) 中掩码Barrett约简的通用机器验证基数界，对应1比特泄露解释，填补了素数域一阶算术掩码下Barrett约简形式化安全分析的空白。

Abstract: Barrett reduction is the nonlinear core of every practical NTT-based post-quantum cryptography implementation. Existing composition frameworks (ISW, t-SNI, PINI, DOM) address Boolean masking over GF(2); none provides a machine-checked characterization of Barrett's leakage under first-order arithmetic masking and the first-order probing model over prime fields. Building on our prior series, QANARY [15], partial-NTT-masking margins [14], algebraic foundations [16], and butterfly composition [18], we close this gap. We prove a trichotomy: for any $q > 0$ and shift $s$, the Barrett internal wire map $f_x(m) = ((x + 2^s - m) \bmod 2^s) \bmod q$ has preimage cardinality in $\{0, 1, 2\}$, never more. We call this the 1-Bit Barrier: max-multiplicity 2 implies at most 1 bit of min-entropy loss per internal wire, universal over all moduli. The count-zero cases, unreachable output values, reveal that actual leakage is often strictly less than 1 bit, making the bound conservative. We introduce PF-PINI (Prime-Field PINI): Barrett satisfies PF-PINI(2); the Cooley-Tukey butterfly satisfies PF-PINI(1). We observe (not yet proved) that with fresh inter-stage masking, the composed pipeline has max-multiplicity $\max(k_1, k_2)$, so the 1-Bit Barrier propagates. The trichotomy, the PF-PINI instantiations, and cardinality results are machine-checked in Lean 4 with Mathlib: 12 proved results, zero sorry, universal over all $q > 0$ (the min-entropy bound follows by standard definitions). Adams Bridge lacks fresh inter-stage masking, violating PF-PINI composition and explaining why Papers 1 [15] and 2 [14] found vulnerabilities. NIST IR 8547 recommends formal methods for PQC implementation validation. The 1-Bit Barrier provides the first universal machine-checked cardinality bound for masked Barrett reduction in ML-KEM (FIPS 203) and ML-DSA (FIPS 204), with a corresponding 1-bit leakage interpretation.

</details>
